   106→    // Calculate dynamic max values for sliders based on combined constraint:
   107→    //
   108→    // REPLACE mode CONSTRAINT (to avoid double-blending artifacts):
   109→    //   min_clip_frames ≥ gap_frame_count + 2 × context_frame_count
   110→    //
   111→    // Rearranged:
   112→    //   max_gap = shortest_clip - 2 × context

   114→    //
   115→    // In INSERT mode, each clip only needs: context frames
   116→    const { maxGapFrames, maxContextFrames, minClipFramesRequired } = useMemo(() => {
   117→        // Standard limits (without clip constraints)
   118→        const standardMaxTotal = 81; // 4N+1 format max
   119→        const standardMaxContext = 30;
   120→
   121→        // Calculate minimum frames required per clip based on current settings
   122→        const minRequired = replaceMode
   123→            ? gapFrames + 2 * contextFrames  // gap + 2*context
   124→            : contextFrames;                  // just context for INSERT mode
   125→
   126→        // Default case: no clip info available
   127→        if (!shortestClipFrames || shortestClipFrames <= 0) {
   128→            const defaultMaxGap = Math.max(1, standardMaxTotal - (contextFrames * 2));
   129→            return {
   130→                maxGapFrames: defaultMaxGap,
   131→                maxContextFrames: standardMaxContext,
   132→                minClipFramesRequired: minRequired,
   133→            };
   134→        }
   135→
   136→        if (replaceMode) {
   137→            // REPLACE mode: min_clip_frames ≥ gap + 2*context
   138→            // max_gap = shortest_clip - 2*context
   139→            const maxGapForClip = Math.max(1, shortestClipFrames - 2 * contextFrames);
   140→            const maxGapForTotal = Math.max(1, standardMaxTotal - (contextFrames * 2));
   141→            let finalMaxGap = Math.min(maxGapForClip, maxGapForTotal);
   142→            // Quantize to valid 4N+1 value

   147→            const maxContextForTotal = Math.floor((standardMaxTotal - gapFrames) / 2);

   149→

   151→                maxGapFrames: Math.max(1, finalMaxGap),
   152→                maxContextFrames: finalMaxContext,
   153→                minClipFramesRequired: minRequired,
   154→            };
   155→        } else {

   159→            const maxGapForTotal = Math.max(1, standardMaxTotal - (contextFrames * 2));
   160→
   161→            return {
   162→                maxGapFrames: maxGapForTotal,
   163→                maxContextFrames: finalMaxContext,

   448→                            {(showResolutionToggle || showFpsToggle || setNoisedInputVideo) && (
   449→                                <Collapsible
   450→                                    open={isAdvancedOpen}
   451→                                    onOpenChange={setIsAdvancedOpen}
   452→                                    className="col-span-2"
   453→                                >
   454→                                    <CollapsibleTrigger asChild>
   455→                                        <Button
   456→                                            variant="outline"
   457→                                            size="sm"
   458→                                            className={cn(
   459→                                                "w-full justify-between px-3 py-2 h-auto border-primary/30 text-primary hover:bg-primary/10 hover:text-primary",
   460→                                                isAdvancedOpen && "rounded-b-none"
   461→                                            )}
   462→                                        >
   463→                                            <span className="text-xs font-medium">Advanced</span>
   464→                                            <ChevronDown className={cn(
   465→                                                "h-4 w-4 transition-transform duration-200",
   466→                                                !isAdvancedOpen && "rotate-90"
   467→                                            )} />
   468→                                        </Button>
   469→                                    </CollapsibleTrigger>
   470→                                    <CollapsibleContent className="">
   471→                                        <div className="border border-t-0 rounded-b-lg p-4 bg-muted/30">
   472→                                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-6">
   473→                                                {/* Resolution Source */}
   474→                                                {showResolutionToggle && (
   475→                                                    <div className="space-y-3">
   476→                                                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between h-5">
   477→                                                            <Label className="text-sm font-medium">Output Resolution:</Label>
   478→                                                        </div>
   479→                                                        <TooltipProvider>
   480→                                                            <Tooltip>
   481→                                                                <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   482→                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border rounded-lg p-2 bg-background/50">
   483→                                                                        <span className={cn("text-[10px] sm:text-xs transition-colors whitespace-nowrap", !useInputVideoResolution ? "font-medium text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground")}>Project</span>
   484→                                                                        <Switch
   485→                                                                            id="join-resolution-source"
   486→                                                                            checked={useInputVideoResolution ?? false}
   487→                                                                            onCheckedChange={(val) => {
   488→                                                                                setUseInputVideoResolution?.(val);
   489→                                                                            }}
   490→                                                                        />
   491→                                                                        <span className={cn("text-[10px] sm:text-xs transition-colors whitespace-nowrap", useInputVideoResolution ? "font-medium text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground")}>Input</span>
   492→                                                                    </div>
   493→                                                                </TooltipTrigger>
   494→                                                                <TooltipContent>
   495→                                                                    <p className="max-w-xs text-xs">
   496→                                                                        Choose whether to use the project's aspect ratio or match the first input video's resolution.
   497→                                                                    </p>
   498→                                                                </TooltipContent>
   499→                                                            </Tooltip>
   500→                                                        </TooltipProvider>
   501→                                                    </div>
   502→                                                )}
   503→
   504→                                                {/* FPS Source */}
   505→                                                {showFpsToggle && (
   506→                                                    <div className="space-y-3">
   507→                                                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between h-5">
   508→                                                            <Label className="text-sm font-medium">Output FPS:</Label>
   509→                                                        </div>
   510→                                                        <TooltipProvider>
   511→                                                            <Tooltip>
   512→                                                                <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   513→                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border rounded-lg p-2 bg-background/50">
   514→                                                                        <span className={cn("text-[10px] sm:text-xs transition-colors whitespace-nowrap", !useInputVideoFps ? "font-medium text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground")}>Project</span>
   515→                                                                        <Switch
   516→                                                                            id="join-fps-source"
   517→                                                                            checked={useInputVideoFps ?? false}
   518→                                                                            onCheckedChange={(val) => {
   519→                                                                                setUseInputVideoFps?.(val);
   520→                                                                            }}
   521→                                                                        />
   522→                                                                        <span className={cn("text-[10px] sm:text-xs transition-colors whitespace-nowrap", useInputVideoFps ? "font-medium text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground")}>Input</span>
   523→                                                                    </div>
   524→                                                                </TooltipTrigger>
   525→                                                                <TooltipContent>
   526→                                                                    <p className="max-w-xs text-xs">
   527→                                                                        Choose whether to use the project's FPS (16 FPS) or keep the input video's original frame rate.
   528→                                                                    </p>
   529→                                                                </TooltipContent>
   530→                                                            </Tooltip>
   531→                                                        </TooltipProvider>
   532→                                                    </div>
   533→                                                )}
   534→
   535→                                                {/* Noised Input Video */}
   536→                                                {setNoisedInputVideo && (
   537→                                                    <div className="space-y-3 col-span-2 mt-4">
   538→                                                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   539→                                                            <TooltipProvider>
   540→                                                                <Tooltip>
   541→                                                                    <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   542→                                                                        <Label className="text-sm font-medium flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help">
   543→                                                                            Noised Input Video:
   544→                                                                            <Info className="w-3 h-3 text-muted-foreground" />
   545→                                                                        </Label>
   546→                                                                    </TooltipTrigger>
   547→                                                                    <TooltipContent>
   548→                                                                        <p className="max-w-xs text-xs">
   549→                                                                            Controls how much the original gap frames influence generation. Lower values preserve more of the original motion/structure; higher values allow more creative regeneration.
   550→                                                                        </p>
   551→                                                                    </TooltipContent>
   552→                                                                </Tooltip>
   553→                                                            </TooltipProvider>
   554→                                                            <span className="text-sm font-mono bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 rounded">{noisedInputVideo.toFixed(2)}</span>
   555→                                                        </div>
   556→                                                        <Slider
   557→                                                            id="join-noised-input"
   558→                                                            min={0}
   559→                                                            max={1}
   560→                                                            step={0.05}
   561→                                                            value={noisedInputVideo}
   562→                                                            onValueChange={(value) => setNoisedInputVideo(sliderNumber(value))}
   563→                                                            className="py-2"
   564→                                                        />
   565→                                                    </div>
   566→                                                )}
   567→                                            </div>
   568→                                        </div>
   569→                                    </CollapsibleContent>
   570→                                </Collapsible>
   571→                            )}
   572→                        </div>
   573→                    </div>
   574→
   575→                    {/* Visualization Column */}
   576→                    <div className="h-full flex flex-col">
   577→                        <Visualization
   578→                            gapFrames={gapFrames}
   579→                            contextFrames={contextFrames}
   580→                            replaceMode={replaceMode}
   581→                            keepBridgingImages={keepBridgingImagesValue}
   582→                            clipPairs={clipPairs}
   583→                            infoContent={
   584→                                <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
   585→                                    <span className="font-medium">Total generation:</span>{' '}
   586→                                    <span className="font-mono font-medium">{actualTotal}</span> frames
   587→                                    {quantizedTotal !== actualTotal && (
   588→                                        <span className="text-muted-foreground/70"> → {quantizedTotal} (4N+1)</span>
   589→                                    )}
   590→                                    {shortestClipFrames && shortestClipFrames > 0 && (
   591→                                        <>
   592→                                            <span className="mx-2">•</span>
   593→                                            {shortestClipFrames > 81 ? (
   594→                                                <>
   595→                                                    <span className="font-medium">Constrained by max generation:</span>{' '}
   596→                                                    <span className="font-mono">81</span> frames
   597→                                                </>
   598→                                            ) : (
   599→                                                <>
   600→                                                    <span className="font-medium">Constrained by shortest clip:</span>{' '}
   601→                                                    <span className="font-mono">{shortestClipFrames}</span> frames
   602→                                                </>
   603→                                            )}
   604→                                            <span className="mx-2">•</span>
   605→                                            <span className="font-medium">Min required:</span>{' '}
   606→                                            <span className={cn(
   607→                                                "font-mono",
   608→                                                minClipFramesRequired > shortestClipFrames && "text-red-600 dark:text-red-400",
   609→                                                minClipFramesRequired > shortestClipFrames * 0.9 && minClipFramesRequired <= shortestClipFrames && "text-yellow-600 dark:text-yellow-400"
   610→                                            )}>
   611→                                                {minClipFramesRequired}
   612→                                            </span>
   613→                                            {' '}frames per clip
   614→                                            {replaceMode && (
   615→                                                <span className="text-muted-foreground/70 ml-1">
   616→                                                    ({gapFrames} gap + 2×{contextFrames} context)
   617→                                                </span>
   618→                                            )}
   619→                                        </>
   620→                                    )}
   621→                                </div>
   622→                            }
   623→                        />
   624→                    </div>
   625→                </div>
   626→            </div>
   627→
   628→            {showGenerateButton && (
   629→                <>
   630→                    <div className="h-px bg-border/50" />
   631→
   632→                    {/* Generate Button */}
   633→                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 pt-4">
   634→                        <Button
   635→                            onClick={onGenerate}
   636→                            disabled={isGenerateDisabled || isGenerating || generateSuccess}
   637→                            className={cn("w-full max-w-md shadow-lg gap-2 h-12",
   638→                                generateSuccess && "bg-green-500 hover:bg-green-600"
   639→                            )}
   640→                            size="lg"
   641→                        >
   642→                            {isGenerating ? (
   643→                                <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />
   644→                            ) : generateSuccess ? (
   645→                                <Check className="w-5 h-5" />
   646→                            ) : (
   647→                                <Film className="w-5 h-5" />
   648→                            )}
   649→                            <span className="font-medium text-lg">
   650→                                {generateSuccess ? 'Task Created' : generateButtonText}
   651→                            </span>
   652→                        </Button>
   653→                    </div>
   654→                </>
   655→            )}
   656→        </div>
   657→    );
   658→};
   659→

     9→import type { SegmentPreviewProps } from './types';
    10→
    11→const FRAMES_PER_SECOND = 16;

    13→export const SegmentPreview: React.FC<SegmentPreviewProps> = ({

    44→  const [imageError, setImageError] = useState(false);
    45→  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState(0);
    46→  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(0);

    50→  const thumbUrl = child.thumbUrl || child.location;
    51→  const videoUrl = child.location;
    52→
    53→  const previewStyle = useMemo(() => {
    54→    const baseWidth = compact ? 180 : 240;
    55→    if (!projectAspectRatio) {
    56→      return { width: baseWidth, aspectRatio: '16/9' };
    57→    }
    58→
    59→    const [w, h] = projectAspectRatio.split(':').map(Number);
    60→    if (w && h) {
    61→      return { width: baseWidth, aspectRatio: `${w}/${h}` };
    62→    }
    63→
    64→    return { width: baseWidth, aspectRatio: '16/9' };
    65→  }, [projectAspectRatio, compact]);
    66→
    67→  const estimatedPreviewHeight = useMemo(() => {

    69→    if (!projectAspectRatio) {
    70→      return Math.round((baseWidth * 9) / 16);
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    const [w, h] = projectAspectRatio.split(':').map(Number);
    74→    if (w && h) {
    75→      return Math.round((baseWidth * h) / w);
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    return Math.round((baseWidth * 9) / 16);
    79→  }, [projectAspectRatio, compact]);
    80→
    81→  const containerBorderClasses =
    82→    layout === 'flow' ? 'border-[3px] border-primary ring-2 ring-primary/20 shadow-lg' : 'border-2 border-primary/30 shadow-md';
    83→
    84→  const handleMouseEnter = useCallback(
    85→    (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    86→      if (isMobile) {
    87→        return;
    88→      }
    89→
    90→      setIsHovering(true);
    91→      const target = e.currentTarget as HTMLElement;

    96→      scrubbingContainerProps?.onMouseEnter?.();

    98→    [isMobile, onScrubbingStart, scrubbingContainerProps],

   100→

   104→        return;
   105→      }
   106→

   112→
   113→  const handleMouseLeave = useCallback(() => {

   147→

   174→        onClick={() => {

   241→
   242→        {isPending && (

1→const FRAME_QUANTIZATION_STEP = 4;
     2→const FRAME_QUANTIZATION_OFFSET = 1;
     3→
     4→export function quantizeTotalFrames(total: number, minTotal: number = 17): number {
     5→  const quantized =
     6→    Math.round((total - FRAME_QUANTIZATION_OFFSET) / FRAME_QUANTIZATION_STEP) *
     7→      FRAME_QUANTIZATION_STEP +
     8→    FRAME_QUANTIZATION_OFFSET;
     9→  return Math.max(minTotal, quantized);
    10→}
    11→
    12→export function getQuantizedGap(
    13→  desiredGap: number,
    14→  context: number,
    15→  minTotal: number = 17
    16→): number {
    17→  const total = context * 2 + desiredGap;
    18→  const quantizedTotal = quantizeTotalFrames(total, minTotal);
    19→  const gap = quantizedTotal - context * 2;
    20→
    21→  if (gap >= 1) {
    22→    return gap;
    23→  }
    24→
    25→  const minimumTotalWithPositiveGap = context * 2 + 1;
    26→  const validTotal = quantizeTotalFrames(minimumTotalWithPositiveGap, minTotal);
    27→  return validTotal - context * 2;
    28→}
    29→
```

    37→import { JoinClipsSettingsForm, type ClipPairInfo, DEFAULT_JOIN_CLIPS_PHASE_CONFIG } from '@/shared/components/JoinClipsSettingsForm';

    40→type JoinSettingsState = ReturnType<typeof useJoinClipsSettings>;

    44→
    45→function useSyncJoinClipsLoras(
    46→  settingsLoaded: boolean,
    47→  selectedLoras: LoraManagerState['selectedLoras'],
    48→  joinSettings: JoinSettingsState,
    49→) {
    50→  const lorasSyncStateRef = useRef<{ lastSyncedKey: string }>({ lastSyncedKey: '' });
    51→
    52→  useEffect(() => {
    53→    if (!settingsLoaded) return;
    54→
    55→    const lorasKey = selectedLoras
    56→      .map(lora => `${lora.id}:${lora.strength}`)
    57→      .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))
    58→      .join(',');
    59→
    60→    if (lorasKey === lorasSyncStateRef.current.lastSyncedKey) return;
    61→
    62→    lorasSyncStateRef.current.lastSyncedKey = lorasKey;
    63→    joinSettings.updateField('loras', selectedLoras.map(lora => ({
    64→      id: lora.id,
    65→      strength: lora.strength,
    66→    })));
    67→  }, [settingsLoaded, selectedLoras, joinSettings]);
    68→}
    69→
    70→function useEnsureKeepBridgingImages(
    71→  keepBridgingImages: boolean | undefined,
    72→  settingsLoaded: boolean,
    73→  joinSettings: JoinSettingsState,
    74→) {
    75→  useEffect(() => {
    76→    if (keepBridgingImages === undefined && settingsLoaded) {
    77→      joinSettings.updateField('keepBridgingImages', false);
    78→    }
    79→  }, [keepBridgingImages, settingsLoaded, joinSettings]);
    80→}
    81→
    82→function useJoinValidationResult(
    83→  clips: ClipManagerState['clips'],
    84→  contextFrameCount: number,
    85→  gapFrameCount: number,
    86→  replaceMode: boolean,
    87→  useInputVideoFps: boolean,
    88→): ValidationResult | null {
    89→  return useMemo(() => {
    90→    const validClips = clips.filter(clip => clip.url);
    91→    if (validClips.length < 2) return null;
    92→
    93→    const stillLoading = validClips.some(clip => clip.metadataLoading || clip.durationSeconds === undefined);
    94→    if (stillLoading) return null;
    95→
    96→    const clipFrameInfos: ClipFrameInfo[] = validClips.map((clip, index) => ({
    97→      index,
    98→      name: `Clip #${index + 1}`,
    99→      frameCount: clip.durationSeconds
   100→        ? calculateEffectiveFrameCount(clip.durationSeconds, useInputVideoFps)
   101→        : 0,
   102→      durationSeconds: clip.durationSeconds,
   103→      source: clip.durationSeconds ? 'estimated' : 'unknown',
   104→    }));
   105→
   106→    return validateClipsForJoin(
   107→      clipFrameInfos,
   108→      contextFrameCount,
   109→      gapFrameCount,
   110→      replaceMode,
   111→    );
   112→  }, [clips, contextFrameCount, gapFrameCount, replaceMode, useInputVideoFps]);
   113→}
   114→
   115→function useJoinClipPairs(
   116→  clips: ClipManagerState['clips'],
   117→  useInputVideoFps: boolean,
   118→): ClipPairInfo[] {
   119→  return useMemo(() => {
   120→    const validClips = clips.filter(clip => clip.url);
   121→    if (validClips.length < 2) return [];
   122→
   123→    const pairs: ClipPairInfo[] = [];
   124→    for (let i = 0; i < validClips.length - 1; i++) {
   125→      const clipA = validClips[i];
   126→      const clipB = validClips[i + 1];
   127→
   128→      const clipAFrameCount = clipA.durationSeconds
   129→        ? calculateEffectiveFrameCount(clipA.durationSeconds, useInputVideoFps)
   130→        : 0;
   131→      const clipBFrameCount = clipB.durationSeconds
   132→        ? calculateEffectiveFrameCount(clipB.durationSeconds, useInputVideoFps)
   133→        : 0;
   134→
   135→      pairs.push({
   136→        pairIndex: i,
   137→        clipA: {
   138→          name: `Clip ${i + 1}`,
   139→          frameCount: clipAFrameCount,
   140→          finalFrameUrl: clipA.finalFrameUrl,
   141→        },
   142→        clipB: {
   143→          name: `Clip ${i + 2}`,
   144→          frameCount: clipBFrameCount,
   145→          posterUrl: clipB.posterUrl,
   146→        },
   147→      });
   148→    }
   149→
   150→    return pairs;
   151→  }, [clips, useInputVideoFps]);
   152→}
   153→
   154→function useRefreshOnVisibility(
   155→  selectedProjectId: string | null,
   156→  queryClient: ReturnType<typeof useQueryClient>,
   157→) {
   158→  useEffect(() => {
   159→    const handleVisibilityChange = () => {
   160→      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && selectedProjectId) {
   161→        queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   162→          [REDACTED](selectedProjectId),
   163→        });
   164→      }
   165→    };
   166→
   167→    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);
   168→    return () => document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);
   169→  }, [selectedProjectId, queryClient]);
   170→}
   171→
   172→function JoinClipsSkeletonGrid({ cachedClipsCount }: { cachedClipsCount: number }) {
   173→  return (
   174→    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
   175→      {Array.from({ length: Math.max(2, cachedClipsCount) + 1 }).map((_, index) => (
   176→        <div key={index} className="space-y-3">
   177→          <div className="relative border rounded-lg p-3 space-y-3 bg-card">
   178→            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   179→              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   180→                <Skeleton className="h-5 w-16" />
   181→              </div>
   182→            </div>
   183→            <div className="space-y-2">
   184→              <div className="aspect-video bg-muted rounded-lg border-2 border-dashed border-border flex items-center justify-center">
   185→                <div className="text-center space-y-2">
   186→                  <Skeleton className="h-8 w-8 rounded-full mx-auto" />
   187→                  <Skeleton className="h-4 w-32 mx-auto" />
   188→                  <Skeleton className="h-3 w-24 mx-auto" />
   189→                </div>
   190→              </div>
   191→            </div>
   192→          </div>
   193→        </div>
   194→      ))}
   195→    </div>
   196→  );
   197→}
   198→
   199→interface JoinClipsGridProps {
   200→  joinSettings: JoinSettingsState;
```

   270→function JoinClipsResults({

   306→          allShots={[]}
   307→          onAddToLastShot={async () => false}

   309→          onDelete={handleDeleteGeneration}
   310→          isDeleting={deletingId}
   311→          currentToolType={TOOL_IDS.JOIN_CLIPS}
   312→          defaultFilters={{ mediaType: 'video', toolTypeFilter: true, shotFilter: 'all' }}
   313→          columnsPerRow={3}
   314→          itemsPerPage={isMobile ? 20 : 12}
   315→          config={{

   443→
   444→function JoinClipsPageLayout({
   445→  selectedProjectId,
   446→  projectAspectRatio,
   447→  isMobile,
   448→  joinSettings,
   449→  settingsLoaded,
   450→  availableLoras,
   451→  loraManager,
   452→  clipManager,
   453→  validationResult,
   454→  clipPairs,
   455→  generateState,
   456→  videosData,
   457→  videosLoading,
   458→  videosFetching,
   459→  deletingId,
   460→  handleDeleteGeneration,
   461→  confirmDialogProps,
   462→}: JoinClipsPageLayoutProps) {
   463→  const settings = joinSettings.settings;
   464→  const settingsFormProps = useMemo(() => buildJoinClipsSettingsFormProps({
   465→    settings,
   466→    joinSettings,
   467→    clipManager,
   468→    validationResult,
   469→    clipPairs,
   470→    availableLoras,

   484→  ]);
   485→
   486→  return (
   487→    <PageFadeIn>
   488→      <div className="flex flex-col gap-y-6 pb-6 px-4 max-w-7xl mx-auto pt-6">
   489→        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   490→          <h1 className="text-3xl font-light tracking-tight text-foreground">Join Clips</h1>
   491→        </div>
   492→
   493→        <JoinClipsGrid
   494→          joinSettings={joinSettings}
   495→          clipManager={clipManager}
   496→          settingsLoaded={settingsLoaded}
   497→        />
   498→
   499→        <Card className="p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-sm border">
   500→          <JoinClipsSettingsForm {...settingsFormProps} />
   501→        </Card>
   502→
   503→        <JoinClipsResults
   504→          videosData={videosData}
   505→          videosLoading={videosLoading}
   506→          videosFetching={videosFetching}
   507→          projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
   508→          isMobile={isMobile}
   509→          deletingId={deletingId}
   510→          handleDeleteGeneration={handleDeleteGeneration}
   511→        />

   514→      {clipManager.lightboxClip && (
   515→        <MediaLightbox
   516→          media={{
   517→            id: clipManager.lightboxClip.id,
   518→            imageUrl: clipManager.lightboxClip.url,
   519→            location: clipManager.lightboxClip.url,
   520→            thumbUrl: clipManager.lightboxClip.posterUrl,
   521→            type: 'video',
   522→          }}
   523→          onClose={() => clipManager.setLightboxClip(null)}
   524→          showNavigation={false}
   525→          showDownload
   526→        />
   527→      )}
   528→
   529→      <DeleteGenerationConfirmDialog {...confirmDialogProps} />
   530→    </PageFadeIn>
   531→  );
   532→}
   533→
   534→const JoinClipsPage: React.FC = () => {
   535→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   536→  const { selectedProjectId, projects } = useProject();
   537→  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
   538→
   539→  const currentProject = projects.find(project => project.id === selectedProjectId);
   540→  const projectAspectRatio = currentProject?.aspectRatio;
   541→
   542→  const joinSettings = useJoinClipsSettings(selectedProjectId);
   543→  const settings = joinSettings.settings;
   544→  const settingsLoaded = joinSettings.status !== 'idle' && joinSettings.status !== 'loading';
   545→
   546→  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
   547→  const loraManager = useLoraManager(availableLoras, {
   548→    projectId: selectedProjectId || undefined,
   549→    persistenceScope: 'project',
   550→    enableProjectPersistence: true,
   551→    [REDACTED],
   552→  });
   553→
   554→  useSyncJoinClipsLoras(settingsLoaded, loraManager.selectedLoras, joinSettings);
   555→  useEnsureKeepBridgingImages(settings.keepBridgingImages, settingsLoaded, joinSettings);
   556→
   557→  const createGenerationMutation = useCreateGeneration();
   558→  const clipManager = useClipManager({
   559→    selectedProjectId,
   560→    joinSettings,
   561→    settingsLoaded,
   562→    loopFirstClip: settings.loopFirstClip,
   563→    createGenerationMutation,
   564→  });
   565→
   566→  const validationResult = useJoinValidationResult(
   567→    clipManager.clips,
   568→    settings.contextFrameCount,
   569→    settings.gapFrameCount,
   570→    settings.replaceMode,
   571→    settings.useInputVideoFps,
   572→  );
   573→
   574→  const clipPairs = useJoinClipPairs(clipManager.clips, settings.useInputVideoFps);
   575→
   576→  const generateState = useJoinClipsGenerate({
   577→    selectedProjectId,
   578→    clips: clipManager.clips,
   579→    transitionPrompts: clipManager.transitionPrompts,
   580→    joinSettings,
   581→    loraManager,
   582→    projectAspectRatio,
   583→    validationResult,
   584→  });
   585→
   586→  const generationsQuery = useProjectGenerations(
   587→    selectedProjectId,
   588→    1,
   589→    100,
   590→    !!selectedProjectId,
   591→    {
   592→      toolType: TOOL_IDS.JOIN_CLIPS,
   593→      mediaType: 'video',
   594→    },
   595→    {
   596→      disablePolling: true,
   597→    }
   598→  );
   599→
   600→  const videosData = generationsQuery.data as GenerationsPaginatedResponse | undefined;
   601→  const videosLoading = generationsQuery.isLoading;
   602→  const videosFetching = generationsQuery.isFetching;
   603→
   604→  const { requestDelete: requestDeleteGeneration, confirmDialogProps, deletingId } = useDeleteGenerationWithConfirm();
   605→  const handleDeleteGeneration = useCallback((id: string) => {
   606→    requestDeleteGeneration(id);
   607→  }, [requestDeleteGeneration]);
   608→
   609→  useEffect(() => {
   610→    if (generateState.videosViewJustEnabled && videosData?.items) {
   611→      generateState.setVideosViewJustEnabled(false);
   612→    }
   613→  }, [generateState, videosData?.items]);
   614→
   615→  useRefreshOnVisibility(selectedProjectId, queryClient);
   616→
   617→  if (!selectedProjectId) {
   618→    return (
   619→      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-64">
   620→        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Please select a project first.</p>
   621→      </div>
   622→    );
   623→  }
   624→
   625→  return (
   626→    <JoinClipsPageLayout
   627→      selectedProjectId={selectedProjectId}
   628→      projectAspectRatio={projectAspectRatio}
   629→      isMobile={isMobile}
   630→      joinSettings={joinSettings}
   631→      settingsLoaded={settingsLoaded}
   632→      availableLoras={availableLoras}
   633→      loraManager={loraManager}
   634→      clipManager={clipManager}
   635→      validationResult={validationResult}
   636→      clipPairs={clipPairs}
   637→      generateState={generateState}
   638→      videosData={videosData}
   639→      videosLoading={videosLoading}
   640→      videosFetching={videosFetching}
   641→      deletingId={deletingId}
   642→      handleDeleteGeneration={handleDeleteGeneration}
   643→      confirmDialogProps={confirmDialogProps}
   644→    />
   645→  );
   646→};
   647→
   648→export default JoinClipsPage;
   649→

    45→export function useClipManager({

    51→}: UseClipManagerParams) {
    52→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53→  // State

    55→  const [clips, setClips] = useState<VideoClip[]>([]);
    56→  const [uploadingClipId, setUploadingClipId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    57→  const [lightboxClip, setLightboxClip] = useState<VideoClip | null>(null);
    58→  const [transitionPrompts, setTransitionPrompts] = useState<TransitionPrompt[]>([]);
    59→  const [draggingOverClipId, setDraggingOverClipId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    60→  const [isScrolling, setIsScrolling] = useState(false);
    61→  const [isLoadingPersistedMedia, setIsLoadingPersistedMedia] = useState(false);
    62→  const [cachedClipsCount, setCachedClipsCountState] = useState(() =>
    63→    getCachedClipsCount(selectedProjectId),
    64→  );
    65→
    66→  // Refs
    67→  const hasLoadedFromSettings = useRef(false);
    68→  const loadedForProjectRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    69→  const preloadedPostersRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
    70→  const fileInputRefs = useRef<{ [clipId: string]: HTMLInputElement | null }>({});
    71→  const videoRefs = useRef<{ [clipId: string]: HTMLVideoElement | null }>({});
    72→
    73→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→  // Reset loading state when project changes
    75→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76→  useEffect(() => {
    77→    if (selectedProjectId && selectedProjectId !== loadedForProjectRef.current) {
    78→      hasLoadedFromSettings.current = false;
    79→      loadedForProjectRef.current = selectedProjectId;
    80→      setClips([]);
    81→      setTransitionPrompts([]);
    82→      preloadedPostersRef.current.clear();
    83→      setCachedClipsCountState(getCachedClipsCount(selectedProjectId));
    84→    }
    85→  }, [selectedProjectId]);
    86→
    87→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    90→  useEffect(() => {

    96→          context: 'JoinClipsPage.pendingClipsConsume',
    97→          showToast: false,
    98→        });
    99→        return;
   100→      }
   101→      const actions = result.value;
   102→      if (actions.length > 0) {
   103→        setClips(prev => applyPendingClipActions(prev, actions));
   104→      }
   105→    });
   106→  }, [settingsLoaded]);
   107→
   108→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   110→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→  useEffect(() => {
   112→    if (!selectedProjectId || !settingsLoaded || hasLoadedFromSettings.current) return;
   113→
   114→    hasLoadedFromSettings.current = true;
   115→
   116→    const {
   117→      clips: initialClips,
   118→      transitionPrompts: initialPrompts,
   119→      posterUrlsToPreload,
   120→    } = buildInitialClipsFromSettings(joinSettings.settings);
   121→
   122→    if (initialPrompts.length > 0) {
   123→      setTransitionPrompts(initialPrompts);
   124→    }
   125→
   126→    if (initialClips.length > 0) {
   127→      const clipsToSet = padClipsWithEmptySlots(initialClips);
   128→
   129→      if (posterUrlsToPreload.length > 0) {
   130→        setIsLoadingPersistedMedia(true);
   131→        preloadPosterImages(posterUrlsToPreload, preloadedPostersRef.current).then(() => {
   132→          setClips(clipsToSet);
   133→          setIsLoadingPersistedMedia(false);
   134→        });
   135→      } else {
   136→        setClips(clipsToSet);
   137→      }
   138→    } else {
   139→      setClips([createEmptyClip(), createEmptyClip()]);
   140→    }
   141→  }, [selectedProjectId, joinSettings.settings, settingsLoaded]);
   142→
   143→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   144→  // Persist clips to settings whenever they change
   145→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   146→  useEffect(() => {
   147→    if (!settingsLoaded) return;
   148→
   149→    const clipsToSave = buildClipsToSave(clips);
   150→    setCachedClipsCount(selectedProjectId, clipsToSave.length);
   151→
   152→    const promptsToSave = buildPromptsToSave(clips, transitionPrompts);
   153→
   154→    const currentClipsJson = JSON.stringify(joinSettings.settings.clips || []);
   155→    const newClipsJson = JSON.stringify(clipsToSave);
   156→    const currentPromptsJson = JSON.stringify(joinSettings.settings.transitionPrompts || []);
   157→    const newPromptsJson = JSON.stringify(promptsToSave);
   158→
   159→    if (currentClipsJson !== newClipsJson || currentPromptsJson !== newPromptsJson) {
   160→      joinSettings.updateFields({

   163→      });
   164→    }

   166→
   167→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   168→  // Lazy-load duration for clips that have URLs but no duration
   169→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   170→  useEffect(() => {

   172→    if (clipsNeedingDuration.length === 0) return;

   174→    // Mark as loading

   178→          ? { ...clip, metadataLoading: true }

   183→    clipsNeedingDuration.forEach(async clip => {

   212→

   215→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   216→  useEffect(() => {

   227→
   228→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   229→  // Prevent autoplay on mobile
   230→  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   231→  useEffect(() => {
   232→    clips.forEach(clip => {

   234→      if (video) {

   236→        video.addEventListener('play', preventPlay);
   237→        video.pause();
   238→        return () => video.removeEventListener('play', preventPlay);
   239→      }
   240→    });
   241→  }, [clips]);

   246→  useEffect(() => {

   250→      return () => clearTimeout(timer);

Perfect! Now I have a complete understanding. Let me create my comprehensive exploration report:

## Join Clips Tool: Segment Display & Loading Analysis

I've completed a thorough exploration of the Join Clips tool. Here's what I found:

### 1. **How Segments Are Displayed at the Top (Visualization Component)**

The segment preview appears in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/JoinClipsSettingsForm/Visualization.tsx` (lines 1-502). It displays a **visual representation** of how transitions work, not actual video segments. Key aspects:

**What's shown:**
- A horizontal bar (height: 80px) visualizing the clip structure
- Color-coded sections: blue (Clip A), yellow (gap/generated), green (Clip B)
- Pair selector buttons when multiple clip pairs exist (lines 65-82)
- Thumbnails of first frame of Clip B and last frame of Clip A (lines 85-113)
- Detailed tooltips for each section with frame counts

**How pairs are determined** (lines 115-151 in `JoinClipsPage.tsx`):
```typescript
function useJoinClipPairs(clips, useInputVideoFps) {
  const validClips = clips.filter(clip => clip.url);  // Only clips with video
  if (validClips.length < 2) return [];  // Empty if < 2 clips
  
  // Creates N-1 pairs from N clips
  for (let i = 0; i < validClips.length - 1; i++) {
    pairs.push({
      clipA: clips[i],
      clipB: clips[i+1]
    });
  }
}
```

**Critical insight:** The visualization only shows data if there are **2 or more clips with URLs**. Otherwise `clipPairs` is empty.

---

### 2. **Skeleton/Placeholder States for Segments**

Segments DON'T use skeleton loaders in the visualization—the clips themselves (in the grid above) do. There are three segment component states:

**A. SegmentPlaceholder** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/inline-segment-video/SegmentPlaceholder.tsx`):
- Shows when a segment is ready to be generated but hasn't started
- Three states (lines 15-81):
  1. **Pending**: Dashed border with loader if `isPending === true` (line 15)
  2. **ReadOnly**: Empty dashed box if `readOnly === true` (line 36)
  3. **Generate button**: Otherwise shows "Generate" with sparkle icon (line 50)

**B. SegmentProcessing** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/inline-segment-video/SegmentProcessing.tsx`):
- Shows when regeneration needed due to source change
- Amber/warning color styling
- Text: "Processing..." if `isPending === true`, else "Regenerate"

**C. SegmentPreview** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/inline-segment-video/SegmentPreview.tsx`):
- Shows the actual rendered segment video
- Displays thumbnail (line 186) or animated pulse loader (line 197) if no image
- Includes play button, variant badge, delete button, progress bar on hover

**Important:** These are NOT used in the Visualization component—they're part of a different system (segment gallery in other tools like travel-between-images).

---

### 3. **How Loop Videos Affect Segment Count**

The `loopFirstClip` setting creates a dramatic UX change:

**Without loop** (normal mode):
- User uploads N clips
- System creates N-1 pairs/segments (each pair = one transition)
- Visualization shows N-1 transition previews

**With loop enabled** (`loopFirstClip: true`):
- Only valid with **exactly 1 clip**
- Creates a "self-transition" where Clip A = Clip B (same video loops)
- Clip count logic in `useJoinClipsGenerate.ts` (lines 59-61, 196-202):

```typescript
const validClips = clips.filter(c => c.url);
const isLooping = loopFirstClip && validClips.length === 1;

// Button text changes:
// isLooping → "Generate Loop"
// !isLooping → "Generate N transitions"
```

**Critical segment count change:**
- When user toggles loop on a **single clip**, segment pair count changes from **0 → 0** (still no pairs shown)
- The Visualization component returns `[]` because it needs 2 distinct clips
- **BUT** the task creation logic (line 82-84) dupes the single clip:
  ```typescript
  const clipsForTask = isLooping
    ? [{ url: validClips[0].url }, { url: validClips[0].url }]  // Duplicate!
    : validClips.map(...)
  ```

---

### 4. **Logic Determining Segments to Show vs Skeletons**

The key filtering happens in `useJoinClipPairs` and the grid skeleton logic:

**In JoinClipsPage.tsx (lines 172-197):**
```typescript
function JoinClipsSkeletonGrid({ cachedClipsCount }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
      {Array.from({ length: Math.max(2, cachedClipsCount) + 1 }).map(...)}
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Grid shows skeleton if** (lines 206-212):
```typescript
const showSkeleton = 
  joinSettings.status === 'loading' ||
  clipManager.isLoadingPersistedMedia ||
  (settingsLoaded && joinSettings.settings?.clips?.length > 0 && 
   clipManager.clips.length === 0);
```

**Visualization appears even with skeleton grid**, but:
- `clipPairs` stays empty `[]` until 2+ clips with URLs exist
- Pair selector doesn't render (line 62: `hasPairs` check)
- Visual bar still renders but shows empty state

**Metadata loading causes count changes** (useClipManager.ts lines 171-211):
- When clip duration loads, it marks `metadataLoading: true` during fetch
- During this state, `isGenerateDisabled` becomes `true` (useJoinClipsGenerate.ts line 208)
- User sees "0 transitions" → some loading → back to "0" or "N transitions"

---

### 5. **Where the "Flash" Could Occur**

Based on code analysis, potential visual flashes:

**A. Pair Count Flash** (most likely):
- Happens when `clipPairs` length changes
- Occurs when:
  1. Second clip metadata loads (e.g., duration finishes loading) → segment appears
  2. User enables/disables loop → changes from 0 pairs to 0 pairs but generation mode changes
  3. User deletes a clip → pair count drops from N-1 to N-2

**B. Visualization Re-renders** (medium probability):
- The `Visualization` component re-renders when:
  - `clipPairs` changes (line 19 prop)
  - Gap/context frames change (affecting visual proportions)
  - Replace mode toggles
- No memoization on the component itself

**C. Settings Form Props Rebuild** (lower probability):
- `buildJoinClipsSettingsFormProps` (lines 370-442) rebuilds on every dependency change
- Line 464-484: Very long dependency array including `clipPairs`

---

### 6. **Key Files & Line References**

| Component | File | Lines | Purpose |
|-----------|------|-------|---------|
| Visualization | `JoinClipsSettingsForm/Visualization.tsx` | 1-502 | Visual bar showing transition structure |
| JoinClipsSettingsForm | `JoinClipsSettingsForm/JoinClipsSettingsForm.tsx` | 1-658 | Settings + visualization container |
| JoinClipsPage | `join-clips/pages/JoinClipsPage.tsx` | 534-648 | Main page layout & data orchestration |
| useJoinClipPairs | `join-clips/pages/JoinClipsPage.tsx` | 115-151 | Converts clips array to pairs |
| useClipManager | `join-clips/hooks/useClipManager.ts` | 45-300+ | Manages clip state, loading, duration |
| SegmentPlaceholder | `inline-segment-video/SegmentPlaceholder.tsx` | 1-81 | Empty slot/pending state (not used in Join Clips visualization) |

---

### 7. **No Explicit "Segment Count" — It's Pairs-Based**

Important clarification: The Join Clips tool doesn't have a traditional "segment count" like the video travel tool. Instead:
- **Pairs** = transitions to generate
- 1 clip = 0 transitions (or 1 if looping)
- 2 clips = 1 transition
- 3 clips = 2 transitions
- N clips = N-1 transitions

The visualization updates based on **valid clip count** (clips with URLs), not placeholders.

     3→import {
     4→  MediaDisplayWithCanvas,
     5→  TopRightControls,
     6→  BottomLeftControls,
     7→  BottomRightControls,

    10→  AnnotationFloatingControls,

    23→// ============================================================================

    37→interface InlineEditCanvasProps {
    38→  variant: 'mobile' | 'desktop';
    39→  model: InlineEditCanvasModel;
    40→  media: GenerationRow;
    41→}
    42→
    43→interface InlineEditCanvasModel {
    44→  imageEditValue: InlineEditStateResult['imageEditValue'];
    45→  media: {
    46→    effectiveImageUrl: string;
    47→    isVideo: boolean;
    48→    imageDimensions: InlineEditStateResult['canvasEnvironment']['imageDimensions'];
    49→    setImageDimensions: InlineEditStateResult['canvasEnvironment']['setImageDimensions'];
    50→  };
    51→  annotation: {

    53→    isAnnotateMode: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['isAnnotateMode'];
    54→    brushStrokes: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['brushStrokes'];
    55→    getDeleteButtonPosition: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['getDeleteButtonPosition'];
    56→    handleToggleFreeForm: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['handleToggleFreeForm'];
    57→    handleDeleteSelected: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['handleDeleteSelected'];
    58→  };
    59→  generation: {
    60→    handleDownload: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleDownload'];

    67→
    68→function buildInlineEditCanvasModel(state: InlineEditStateResult): InlineEditCanvasModel {
    69→  return {
    70→    imageEditValue: state.imageEditValue,
    71→    media: {
    72→      effectiveImageUrl: state.canvasEnvironment.effectiveImageUrl,
    73→      isVideo: state.canvasEnvironment.isVideo,
    74→      imageDimensions: state.canvasEnvironment.imageDimensions,
    75→      setImageDimensions: state.canvasEnvironment.setImageDimensions,
    76→    },
    77→    annotation: {
    78→      selectedShapeId: state.inpaintingState.selectedShapeId,
    79→      isAnnotateMode: state.inpaintingState.isAnnotateMode,
    80→      brushStrokes: state.inpaintingState.brushStrokes,
    81→      getDeleteButtonPosition: state.inpaintingState.getDeleteButtonPosition,
    82→      handleToggleFreeForm: state.inpaintingState.handleToggleFreeForm,
    83→      handleDeleteSelected: state.inpaintingState.handleDeleteSelected,
    84→    },
    85→    generation: {
    86→      handleDownload: state.generationState.handleDownload,
    87→      localStarred: state.generationState.localStarred,
    88→      handleToggleStar: state.generationState.handleToggleStar,
    89→      toggleStarPending: state.generationState.toggleStarMutation.isPending,
    90→    },
    91→    isSpecialEditMode: state.inpaintingState.isSpecialEditMode,
    92→  };
    93→}
    94→
    95→function InlineEditCanvas({ variant, model, media }: InlineEditCanvasProps) {
    96→  const isMobileVariant = variant === 'mobile';
    97→
    98→  return (
    99→    <ImageEditProvider value={model.imageEditValue}>
   100→      <MediaDisplayWithCanvas
   101→        effectiveImageUrl={model.media.effectiveImageUrl}
   102→        thumbUrl={media.thumbnail_url || media.thumbUrl}
   103→        isVideo={model.media.isVideo}
   104→        onImageLoad={model.media.setImageDimensions}
   105→        variant={isMobileVariant ? "mobile-stacked" : "desktop-side-panel"}
   106→        containerClassName={isMobileVariant ? "w-full h-full" : "max-w-full max-h-full"}
   107→        debugContext={isMobileVariant ? "Mobile Inline" : "InlineEdit"}
   108→        imageDimensions={model.media.imageDimensions}
   109→      />
   110→
   111→      {!isMobileVariant && (
   112→        <AnnotationFloatingControls
   113→          selectedShapeId={model.annotation.selectedShapeId}
   114→          isAnnotateMode={model.annotation.isAnnotateMode}
   115→          brushStrokes={model.annotation.brushStrokes}
   116→          getDeleteButtonPosition={model.annotation.getDeleteButtonPosition}
   117→          onToggleFreeForm={model.annotation.handleToggleFreeForm}
   118→          onDeleteSelected={model.annotation.handleDeleteSelected}
   119→          positionStrategy="absolute"
   120→        />
   121→      )}
   122→
   123→      {model.isSpecialEditMode && (
   124→        <FloatingToolControls
   125→          variant={isMobileVariant ? "mobile" : "tablet"}
   126→        />
   127→      )}
   128→
   129→      <TopRightControls
   130→        showDownload={true}
   131→        handleDownload={model.generation.handleDownload}
   132→      />
   133→
   134→      <BottomLeftControls
   135→        localStarred={model.generation.localStarred}
   136→        handleToggleStar={model.generation.handleToggleStar}
   137→        toggleStarPending={model.generation.toggleStarPending}
   138→      />
   139→
   140→      <BottomRightControls
   141→        showAddToReferences={false}
   142→      />
   143→    </ImageEditProvider>
   144→  );
   145→}
   146→
   147→// ============================================================================
   148→// InlineEditSidebar — EditModePanel or fallback placeholder
   149→// ============================================================================
   150→
   151→interface InlineEditSidebarProps {
   152→  variant: 'mobile' | 'desktop';
   153→  model: InlineEditSidebarModel;
   154→  onClose: () => void;
   155→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string) => Promise<void>;
   156→}
   157→
   158→interface InlineEditSidebarModel {
   159→  isSpecialEditMode: boolean;
   160→  sourceGenerationData: InlineEditStateResult['sourceGenerationData'];
   161→  currentMediaId: string;
   162→  generation: {
   163→    handleUnifiedGenerate: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleUnifiedGenerate'];
   164→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit'];
   165→    handleGenerateReposition: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleGenerateReposition'];
   166→    handleSaveAsVariant: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleSaveAsVariant'];
   167→    handleGenerateImg2Img: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleGenerateImg2Img'];
   168→    img2imgLoraManager: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['img2imgLoraManager'];
   169→  };
   170→  availableLoras: InlineEditStateResult['availableLoras'];
   171→  imageEditValue: InlineEditStateResult['imageEditValue'];
   172→  variants: {
   173→    list: InlineEditStateResult['variants']['variants'];
   174→    activeVariantId: string | null;
   175→    isLoading: boolean;
   176→    setActiveVariantId: InlineEditStateResult['variants']['setActiveVariantId'];
   177→    setPrimaryVariant: InlineEditStateResult['variants']['setPrimaryVariant'];
   178→    deleteVariant: InlineEditStateResult['variants']['deleteVariant'];
   179→  };
   180→  actions: {
   181→    enterInpaintMode: () => void;
   182→    enterMagicEditMode: () => void;
   183→  };
   184→}
   185→
   186→function buildInlineEditSidebarModel(state: InlineEditStateResult): InlineEditSidebarModel {
   187→  return {
   188→    isSpecialEditMode: state.inpaintingState.isSpecialEditMode,
   189→    sourceGenerationData: state.sourceGenerationData,
   190→    currentMediaId: state.media.id,
   191→    generation: {
   192→      handleUnifiedGenerate: state.generationState.handleUnifiedGenerate,
   193→      handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit: state.generationState.handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit,
   194→      handleGenerateReposition: state.generationState.handleGenerateReposition,
   195→      handleSaveAsVariant: state.generationState.handleSaveAsVariant,
   196→      handleGenerateImg2Img: state.generationState.handleGenerateImg2Img,
   197→      img2imgLoraManager: state.generationState.img2imgLoraManager,
   198→    },
   199→    availableLoras: state.availableLoras,
   200→    imageEditValue: state.imageEditValue,
   201→    variants: {
   202→      list: state.variants.variants,
   203→      activeVariantId: state.variants.activeVariant?.id || null,
   204→      isLoading: state.variants.isLoading,
   205→      setActiveVariantId: state.variants.setActiveVariantId,
   206→      setPrimaryVariant: state.variants.setPrimaryVariant,
   207→      deleteVariant: state.variants.deleteVariant,
   208→    },
   209→    actions: {
   210→      enterInpaintMode: () => {
   211→        state.inpaintingState.setIsInpaintMode(true);
   212→        state.inpaintingState.setEditMode('inpaint');
   213→      },
   214→      enterMagicEditMode: state.inpaintingState.handleEnterMagicEditMode,
   215→    },
   216→  };
   217→}
   218→
   219→function InlineEditSidebar({ variant, model, onClose, onNavigateToGeneration }: InlineEditSidebarProps) {
   220→  const sidebarContent = model.isSpecialEditMode ? (
   221→    <EditModePanel
   222→      variant={variant === 'mobile' ? 'mobile' : 'desktop'}
   223→      hideInfoEditToggle={true}
   224→      simplifiedHeader={true}
   225→      sourceGenerationData={model.sourceGenerationData}
   226→      onOpenExternalGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration ?
   227→        async (id) => onNavigateToGeneration(id) : undefined
   228→      }
   229→      currentMediaId={model.currentMediaId}
   230→      handleUnifiedGenerate={model.generation.handleUnifiedGenerate}
   231→      handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit={model.generation.handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit}
   232→      handleGenerateReposition={model.generation.handleGenerateReposition}
   233→      handleSaveAsVariant={model.generation.handleSaveAsVariant}
   234→      handleGenerateImg2Img={model.generation.handleGenerateImg2Img}
   235→      img2imgLoraManager={model.generation.img2imgLoraManager}
   236→      availableLoras={model.availableLoras}
   237→      coreState={{ onClose }}
   238→      imageEditState={model.imageEditValue}
   239→    />
   240→  ) : (
   241→    <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center gap-y-4">
   242→      <h3 className="text-xl font-medium">Image Editor</h3>
   243→      <p className="text-muted-foreground">Select an option to start editing</p>
   244→
   245→      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 w-full max-w-xs">
   246→        <Button onClick={() => {
   247→          model.actions.enterInpaintMode();
   248→        }} className="w-full">
   249→          Inpaint / Erase
   250→        </Button>
   251→
   252→        <Button onClick={model.actions.enterMagicEditMode} variant="secondary" className="w-full">
   253→          Magic Edit
   254→        </Button>
   255→      </div>
   256→    </div>
   257→  );
   258→
   259→  return (
   260→    <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col min-h-0">
   261→      <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto">
   262→        {sidebarContent}
   263→      </div>
   264→      <div
   265→        className={cn(
   266→          'border-t border-border bg-background/95 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 pt-3 pb-6',
   267→          variant === 'mobile' ? 'pb-8' : 'pb-6'
   268→        )}
   269→      >
   270→        <VariantSelector
   271→          variants={model.variants.list}
   272→          activeVariantId={model.variants.activeVariantId}
   273→          onVariantSelect={(variantId) => model.variants.setActiveVariantId(variantId)}
   274→          onMakePrimary={model.variants.setPrimaryVariant}
   275→          isLoading={model.variants.isLoading}
   276→          onDeleteVariant={model.variants.deleteVariant}
   277→        />
   278→      </div>
   279→    </div>
   280→  );
   281→}
   282→
   283→// ============================================================================
   284→// InlineEditView — layout orchestrator (mobile vs desktop)
   285→// ============================================================================
   286→
   287→export function InlineEditView({ media, onClose, onNavigateToGeneration }: InlineEditViewProps) {
   288→  const state = useInlineEditState(media, onNavigateToGeneration);
   289→  const canvasModel = buildInlineEditCanvasModel(state);
   290→  const sidebarModel = buildInlineEditSidebarModel(state);
   291→  const isMobile = state.canvasEnvironment.isMobile;
   292→
   293→  if (isMobile) {
   294→    return (
   295→      <TooltipProvider delayDuration={500}>
   296→        <div className="w-full flex flex-col bg-transparent">
   297→          <div
   298→              className="flex items-center justify-center relative bg-black w-full shrink-0 rounded-t-2xl overflow-hidden"
   299→              style={{ height: '45dvh', touchAction: 'pan-y' }}
   300→            >
   301→              <InlineEditCanvas variant="mobile" model={canvasModel} media={media} />
   302→            </div>
   303→
   304→            <div
   305→              className={cn(
   306→                "bg-background border-t border-border relative z-[60] w-full rounded-b-2xl pb-8"
   307→              )}
   308→              style={{ minHeight: '55dvh' }}
   309→            >
   310→              <InlineEditSidebar
   311→                variant="mobile"
   312→                model={sidebarModel}
   313→                onClose={onClose}
   314→                onNavigateToGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration}
   315→              />
   316→            </div>
   317→          </div>
   318→        </TooltipProvider>
   319→    );
   320→  }
   321→
   322→  return (
   323→    <TooltipProvider delayDuration={500}>
   324→      <div className="w-full h-full flex bg-transparent overflow-hidden">
   325→        <div
   326→            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center relative bg-black rounded-l-xl overflow-hidden"
   327→            style={{ width: '60%', height: '100%' }}
   328→          >
   329→            <InlineEditCanvas variant="desktop" model={canvasModel} media={media} />
   330→          </div>
   331→
   332→          <div
   333→            className={cn(
   334→              "bg-background border-l border-border overflow-y-auto relative z-[60] rounded-r-xl"
   335→            )}
   336→            style={{ width: '40%', height: '100%' }}
   337→          >
   338→            <InlineEditSidebar
   339→              variant="desktop"
   340→              model={sidebarModel}
   341→              onClose={onClose}
   342→              onNavigateToGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration}
   343→            />
   344→          </div>
   345→        </div>
   346→      </TooltipProvider>
   347→  );
   348→}
   349→

   391→    setCreateAsGeneration: env.setCreateAsGeneration,
   392→    qwenEditModel: persistence.editSettings.qwenEditModel,
   393→    setQwenEditModel: persistence.editSettings.setQwenEditModel,
   394→    advancedSettings: persistence.editSettings.advancedSettings,
   395→    setAdvancedSettings: persistence.editSettings.setAdvancedSettings,

   399→    img2imgGenerateSuccess: img2img.img2imgGenerateSuccess,

   401→    repositionGenerateSuccess: reposition.repositionGenerateSuccess,

   408→  return {

   414→}
   415→
   416→export interface InlineEditStateResult {
   417→  media: GenerationRow;
   418→  canvasEnvironment: {
   419→    isMobile: boolean;
   420→    selectedProjectId: string | null | undefined;
   421→    isCloudMode: boolean;
   422→    isVideo: boolean;
   423→    imageContainerRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
   424→    effectiveImageUrl: string;
   425→    imageDimensions: { width: number; height: number } | null;
   426→    setImageDimensions: Dispatch<SetStateAction<{ width: number; height: number } | null>>;
   427→    isUpscaling: boolean;
   428→    handleUpscale: () => Promise<void>;
   429→  };
   430→  inpaintingState: {
   431→    isInpaintMode: boolean;
   432→    editMode: InpaintingHookResult['editMode'];
   433→    brushStrokes: InpaintingHookResult['brushStrokes'];
   434→    currentStroke: InpaintingHookResult['currentStroke'];
   435→    isDrawing: boolean;
   436→    isEraseMode: boolean;
   437→    brushSize: number;
   438→    annotationMode: InpaintingHookResult['annotationMode'];
   439→    selectedShapeId: string | null;
   440→    isAnnotateMode: boolean;
   441→    handleKonvaPointerDown: InpaintingHookResult['handleKonvaPointerDown'];
   442→    handleKonvaPointerMove: InpaintingHookResult['handleKonvaPointerMove'];
   443→    handleKonvaPointerUp: InpaintingHookResult['handleKonvaPointerUp'];
   444→    handleShapeClick: InpaintingHookResult['handleShapeClick'];
   445→    strokeOverlayRef: InpaintingHookResult['strokeOverlayRef'];
   446→    getDeleteButtonPosition: InpaintingHookResult['getDeleteButtonPosition'];
   447→    handleToggleFreeForm: InpaintingHookResult['handleToggleFreeForm'];
   448→    handleDeleteSelected: InpaintingHookResult['handleDeleteSelected'];
   449→    handleUndo: InpaintingHookResult['handleUndo'];
   450→    handleClearMask: InpaintingHookResult['handleClearMask'];
   451→    setIsInpaintMode: InpaintingHookResult['setIsInpaintMode'];
   452→    setEditMode: InpaintingHookResult['setEditMode'];
   453→    setBrushSize: InpaintingHookResult['setBrushSize'];
   454→    setIsEraseMode: InpaintingHookResult['setIsEraseMode'];
   455→    setAnnotationMode: InpaintingHookResult['setAnnotationMode'];
   456→    isSpecialEditMode: boolean;
   457→    handleEnterMagicEditMode: MagicEditHookResult['handleEnterMagicEditMode'];
   458→  };
   459→  transformState: {
   460→    repositionTransform: RepositionHookResult['transform'];
   461→    setScale: RepositionHookResult['setScale'];
   462→    setRotation: RepositionHookResult['setRotation'];
   463→    toggleFlipH: RepositionHookResult['toggleFlipH'];
   464→    toggleFlipV: RepositionHookResult['toggleFlipV'];
   465→    resetTransform: RepositionHookResult['resetTransform'];
   466→    getTransformStyle: RepositionHookResult['getTransformStyle'];
   467→  };
   468→  generationState: {
   469→    localStarred: StarToggleHookResult['localStarred'];
   470→    toggleStarMutation: StarToggleHookResult['toggleStarMutation'];
   471→    handleToggleStar: StarToggleHookResult['handleToggleStar'];
   472→    handleDownload: () => Promise<void>;
   473→    handleUnifiedGenerate: MagicEditHookResult['handleUnifiedGenerate'];
   474→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit: InpaintingHookResult['handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit'];
   475→    handleGenerateReposition: RepositionHookResult['handleGenerateReposition'];
   476→    handleSaveAsVariant: RepositionHookResult['handleSaveAsVariant'];
   477→    handleGenerateImg2Img: Img2ImgHookResult['handleGenerateImg2Img'];
   478→    img2imgLoraManager: Img2ImgHookResult['loraManager'];
   479→  };
   480→  variants: {
   481→    variants: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['variants'];
   482→    activeVariant: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['activeVariant'];
   483→    isLoading: boolean;
   484→    setActiveVariantId: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['setActiveVariantId'];
   485→    setPrimaryVariant: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['setPrimaryVariant'];
   486→    deleteVariant: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['deleteVariant'];
   487→  };
   488→  sourceGenerationData: SourceGenerationData;
   489→  availableLoras: PublicLorasData;
   490→  imageEditValue: ImageEditValue;
   491→}
   492→
   493→export function useInlineEditState(
   494→  media: GenerationRow,
   495→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string) => Promise<void>,
   496→): InlineEditStateResult {
   497→  const env = useInlineEditEnvironment(media);
   498→  const persistence = useInlineEditPersistence(env.actualGenerationId, env.selectedProjectId);
   499→  const activeVariant = persistence.variants.activeVariant;
   500→  const effectiveImageUrl = activeVariant?.location || env.effectiveImageUrl;
   501→
   502→  useEffect(() => {
   503→    env.setUpscaleActiveVariant(activeVariant?.location, activeVariant?.id);
   504→  }, [env, activeVariant?.location, activeVariant?.id]);
   505→
   506→  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
   507→  const { inpainting, magic } = useInlineEditInpaintingAndMagic(media, env, persistence);
   508→  const reposition = useInlineEditReposition(media, env, inpainting, magic, persistence);
   509→  const img2img = useInlineEditImg2Img(media, env, persistence, availableLoras);
   510→
   511→  useInlineEditSettingsSync(env.actualGenerationId, persistence.editSettings, inpainting);
   512→
   513→  const { sourceGenerationData } = useSourceGeneration({
   514→    media,
   515→    onOpenExternalGeneration: onNavigateToGeneration
   516→      ? async (id) => onNavigateToGeneration(id)
   517→      : undefined,
   518→  });
   519→
   520→  const starToggle = useStarToggle({ media });
   521→
   522→  const handleDownload = async () => {
   523→    await downloadMedia(effectiveImageUrl, media.id, env.isVideo, media.contentType);
   524→  };
   525→
   526→  const {
   527→    inpaintingState,
   528→    transformState,
   529→    generationState,
   530→    imageEditValue,
   531→  } = buildInlineEditStateModels({
   532→    env,
   533→    persistence,
   534→    inpainting,
   535→    magic,
   536→    reposition,
   537→    img2img,
   538→    starToggle,
   539→    handleDownload,
   540→  });
   541→
   542→  return {
   543→    media,
   544→    canvasEnvironment: {
   545→      isMobile: env.isMobile,
   546→      selectedProjectId: env.selectedProjectId,
   547→      isCloudMode: env.isCloudMode,
   548→      isVideo: env.isVideo,
   549→      imageContainerRef: env.imageContainerRef,
   550→      effectiveImageUrl,
   551→      imageDimensions: env.imageDimensions,
   552→      setImageDimensions: env.setImageDimensions,
   553→      isUpscaling: env.isUpscaling,
   554→      handleUpscale: env.handleUpscale,
   555→    },
   556→    inpaintingState,
   557→    transformState,
   558→    generationState,
   559→    variants: {
   560→      variants: persistence.variants.variants,
   561→      activeVariant: persistence.variants.activeVariant,
   562→      isLoading: persistence.variants.isLoading,
   563→      setActiveVariantId: persistence.variants.setActiveVariantId,
   564→      setPrimaryVariant: persistence.variants.setPrimaryVariant,
   565→      deleteVariant: persistence.variants.deleteVariant,
   566→    },
   567→    sourceGenerationData,
   568→    availableLoras,
   569→    imageEditValue,
   570→  } satisfies InlineEditStateResult;
   571→}
   572→

1→/**
     2→ * MediaSelectionPanel
     3→ *
     4→ * Shared gallery panel for selecting an image or video from the project.
     5→ * Used by EditVideoPage (video selection) and EditImagesPage (image selection).
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import React, { useState } from 'react';
     9→import { LayoutGrid } from 'lucide-react';
    10→import { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    11→import { ReighLoading } from '@/shared/components/ReighLoading';
    12→import { useProject } from '@/shared/contexts/ProjectContext';
    13→import { useProjectGenerations, type GenerationsPaginatedResponse } from '@/shared/hooks/useProjectGenerations';
    14→import MediaGallery, { DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS, type GalleryFilterState } from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery';
    15→import { useListShots } from '@/shared/hooks/shots';
    16→
    17→interface MediaSelectionPanelProps {
    18→  /** Called when the user selects a media item */
    19→  onSelect: (media: GenerationRow) => void;
    20→  /** 'image' or 'video' */
    21→  mediaType: 'image' | 'video';
    22→  /** Header label, e.g. "Select a Video" */
    23→  label?: string;
    24→}
    25→
    26→export function MediaSelectionPanel({ onSelect, mediaType, label }: MediaSelectionPanelProps) {
    27→  const { selectedProjectId } = useProject();
    28→  const [galleryFilters, setGalleryFilters] = useState<GalleryFilterState>({
    29→    ...DEFAULT_GALLERY_FILTERS,
    30→    mediaType,
    31→    toolTypeFilter: mediaType === 'image' ? false : true,
    32→    excludePositioned: false,
    33→  });
    34→  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
    35→  // Reset to page 1 when filters change (prev-value ref avoids useEffect+setState)
    36→  const prevFilterKeyRef = React.useRef(`${galleryFilters.shotFilter}|${galleryFilters.searchTerm}`);
    37→  const filterKey = `${galleryFilters.shotFilter}|${galleryFilters.searchTerm}`;
    38→  if (prevFilterKeyRef.current !== filterKey) {
    39→    prevFilterKeyRef.current = filterKey;
    40→    if (currentPage !== 1) setCurrentPage(1);
    41→  }
    42→  const { data: shots } = useListShots(selectedProjectId);
    43→  const itemsPerPage = 15;
    44→
    45→  const {
    46→    data: generationsData,
    47→    isLoading: isGalleryLoading,
    48→  } = useProjectGenerations(
    49→    selectedProjectId || null,
    50→    currentPage,
    51→    itemsPerPage,
    52→    true,
    53→    {
    54→      shotId: galleryFilters.shotFilter === 'all' ? undefined : galleryFilters.shotFilter,
    55→      mediaType,
    56→      searchTerm: galleryFilters.searchTerm.trim() || undefined
    57→    }
    58→  );
    59→
    60→  const headerText = label ?? (mediaType === 'video' ? 'Select a Video' : 'Select an Image');
    61→
    62→  return (
    63→    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
    64→      <div className="px-6 pt-4 pb-2 border-b">
    65→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">
    66→          <LayoutGrid className="w-4 h-4" />
    67→          {headerText}
    68→        </div>
    69→      </div>
    70→
    71→      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-0 m-0 relative pt-4 px-4 md:px-6">
    72→         {isGalleryLoading && !generationsData ? (
    73→            <ReighLoading />
    74→         ) : (
    75→            <MediaGallery
    76→               images={(generationsData as GenerationsPaginatedResponse | undefined)?.items || []}
    77→               onImageClick={(media) => onSelect(media as GenerationRow)}
    78→               allShots={shots || []}
    79→               filters={galleryFilters}
    80→               onFiltersChange={setGalleryFilters}
    81→               itemsPerPage={itemsPerPage}
    82→               offset={(currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage}
    83→               totalCount={(generationsData as GenerationsPaginatedResponse | undefined)?.total || 0}
    84→               onServerPageChange={setCurrentPage}
    85→               serverPage={currentPage}
    86→               config={{
    87→                 showShotFilter: true,
    88→                 showSearch: true,
    89→                 hideTopFilters: true,
    90→                 hideShotNotifier: true,
    91→                 showDelete: false,
    92→                 showDownload: false,
    93→                 showShare: false,
    94→                 showEdit: false,
    95→                 showStar: false,
    96→                 showAddToShot: false,
    97→                 enableSingleClick: true,
    98→                 hideBottomPagination: true,
    99→                 videosAsThumbnails: mediaType === 'video',
   100→               }}
   101→            />
   102→         )}
   103→      </div>
   104→    </div>
   105→  );
   106→}
   107→

   170→  // Skeleton count: show enough to fill the gap from deleted items

   172→  const computedSkeletonCount = React.useMemo(() => {
   173→    if (!isBackfillLoading || !isServerPagination) return 0;
   174→
   175→    // Calculate optimistic total (server total minus pending deletes)
   176→    const optimisticTotal = Math.max(0, totalCount - optimisticDeletedCount);
   177→
   178→    // How many items should be on this page?
   179→    const itemsAfterOffset = Math.max(0, optimisticTotal - offset);
   180→    const expectedItems = Math.min(itemsPerPage, itemsAfterOffset);
   181→
   182→    // How many items do we actually have?
   183→    const actualItems = paginatedImages.length;
   184→
   185→    // Gap = expected - actual, but always show at least 1 while loading
   186→    // This ensures smooth transition: multiple skeletons during delete,
   187→    // then 1 skeleton remains until image loads
   188→    const gapCount = Math.max(0, expectedItems - actualItems);
