1→import React from 'react';
     2→import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
     3→import { parseRatio } from '@/shared/lib/media/aspectRatios';
     4→
     5→interface SkeletonGalleryProps {
     6→  /** Number of skeleton items to show */
     7→  count?: number;
     8→  /** Number of columns for the grid (responsive breakpoints) */
     9→  columns?: {
    10→    base?: number;
    11→    sm?: number;
    12→    md?: number;
    13→    lg?: number;
    14→    xl?: number;
    15→    '2xl'?: number;
    16→  };
    17→  /** Fixed column count - overrides responsive columns when provided */
    18→  fixedColumns?: number;
    19→  /** Custom gap classes (e.g. "gap-2 sm:gap-3 md:gap-4") - defaults to "gap-4" */
    20→  gapClasses?: string;
    21→  /** Whether to use white text/styling (for dark panes) */
    22→  darkSurface?: boolean;
    23→  /** Custom className for the container */
    24→  className?: string;
    25→  /** Whether to show skeleton controls at the top (pagination, filters, search) */
    26→  showControls?: boolean;
    27→  /** Project aspect ratio (e.g. "16:9", "4:3", "1:1") to match project dimensions */
    28→  projectAspectRatio?: string;
    29→}
    30→
    31→/**
    32→ * A reusable skeleton loading component for gallery layouts.
    33→ * Provides consistent skeleton styling across different tools and pages.
    34→ */
    35→export function SkeletonGallery({
    36→  count = 20,
    37→  columns = { base: 2, sm: 3, md: 4, lg: 5, xl: 6 },
    38→  fixedColumns,
    39→  gapClasses = 'gap-4',
    40→  darkSurface = false,
    41→  className,
    42→  showControls = false,
    43→  projectAspectRatio
    44→}: SkeletonGalleryProps) {
    45→  
    46→  const gridCols = cn(
    47→    'grid',
    48→    gapClasses,
    49→    columns.base === 1 && 'grid-cols-1',
    50→    columns.base === 2 && 'grid-cols-2',
    51→    columns.base === 3 && 'grid-cols-3',
    52→    columns.base === 4 && 'grid-cols-4',
    53→    columns.base === 5 && 'grid-cols-5',
    54→    columns.base === 6 && 'grid-cols-6',
    55→    columns.sm && columns.sm === 1 && 'sm:grid-cols-1',
    56→    columns.sm && columns.sm === 2 && 'sm:grid-cols-2', 
    57→    columns.sm && columns.sm === 3 && 'sm:grid-cols-3',
    58→    columns.sm && columns.sm === 4 && 'sm:grid-cols-4',
    59→    columns.sm && columns.sm === 5 && 'sm:grid-cols-5',
    60→    columns.sm && columns.sm === 6 && 'sm:grid-cols-6',
    61→    columns.md && columns.md === 1 && 'md:grid-cols-1',
    62→    columns.md && columns.md === 2 && 'md:grid-cols-2',
    63→    columns.md && columns.md === 3 && 'md:grid-cols-3', 
    64→    columns.md && columns.md === 4 && 'md:grid-cols-4',
    65→    columns.md && columns.md === 5 && 'md:grid-cols-5',
    66→    columns.md && columns.md === 6 && 'md:grid-cols-6',
    67→    columns.lg && columns.lg === 1 && 'lg:grid-cols-1',
    68→    columns.lg && columns.lg === 2 && 'lg:grid-cols-2',
    69→    columns.lg && columns.lg === 3 && 'lg:grid-cols-3',
    70→    columns.lg && columns.lg === 4 && 'lg:grid-cols-4', 
    71→    columns.lg && columns.lg === 5 && 'lg:grid-cols-5',
    72→    columns.lg && columns.lg === 6 && 'lg:grid-cols-6',
    73→    columns.xl && columns.xl === 1 && 'xl:grid-cols-1',
    74→    columns.xl && columns.xl === 2 && 'xl:grid-cols-2',
    75→    columns.xl && columns.xl === 3 && 'xl:grid-cols-3',
    76→    columns.xl && columns.xl === 4 && 'xl:grid-cols-4',
    77→    columns.xl && columns.xl === 5 && 'xl:grid-cols-5', 
    78→    columns.xl && columns.xl === 6 && 'xl:grid-cols-6',
    79→    columns['2xl'] && columns['2xl'] === 1 && '2xl:grid-cols-1',
    80→    columns['2xl'] && columns['2xl'] === 2 && '2xl:grid-cols-2',
    81→    columns['2xl'] && columns['2xl'] === 3 && '2xl:grid-cols-3',
    82→    columns['2xl'] && columns['2xl'] === 4 && '2xl:grid-cols-4',
    83→    columns['2xl'] && columns['2xl'] === 5 && '2xl:grid-cols-5',
    84→    columns['2xl'] && columns['2xl'] === 6 && '2xl:grid-cols-6'
    85→  );
    86→
    87→  const skeletonBg = darkSurface ? 'bg-zinc-700/60' : 'bg-muted';
    88→
    89→  // Calculate aspect ratio for skeleton items
    90→  let aspectRatioPadding = '100%'; // Default to square (1:1)
    91→  
    92→  if (projectAspectRatio) {
    93→    const ratio = parseRatio(projectAspectRatio);
    94→    if (!isNaN(ratio)) {
    95→      const calculatedPadding = (1 / ratio) * 100; // height/width * 100
    96→      // Ensure reasonable aspect ratio bounds
    97→      const minPadding = 60; // Minimum 60% height (for very wide images)
    98→      const maxPadding = 200; // Maximum 200% height (for very tall images)
    99→      aspectRatioPadding = `${Math.min(Math.max(calculatedPadding, minPadding), maxPadding)}%`;
   100→    }
   101→  }
   102→
   103→  return (
   104→    <div className={cn('space-y-6 pb-8', className)}>
   105→      {showControls && (
   106→        <div className="mt-0 space-y-3">
   107→          {/* Pagination row - matches MediaGallery pagination section */}
   108→          <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
   109→            {/* Left side - Pagination controls skeleton */}
   110→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   111→              <div className={cn('h-8 w-16 rounded animate-pulse', skeletonBg)} />
   112→              <div className={cn('h-4 w-40 rounded animate-pulse', skeletonBg)} />
   113→              <div className={cn('h-8 w-16 rounded animate-pulse', skeletonBg)} />
   114→            </div>
   115→
   116→            {/* Right side - Starred filter skeleton */}
   117→            <div className="flex items-center gap-x-2">
   118→              <div className={cn('h-4 w-4 rounded animate-pulse', skeletonBg)} />
   119→              <div className={cn('h-4 w-12 rounded animate-pulse', skeletonBg)} />
   120→            </div>
   121→          </div>
   122→
   123→          {/* Filters row - matches MediaGallery filters section */}
   124→          <div className="flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-y-2">
   125→            {/* Left side - Filters skeleton */}
   126→            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
   127→              {/* Shot Filter skeleton */}
   128→              <div className={cn('h-8 w-[140px] rounded animate-pulse', skeletonBg)} />
   129→              
   130→              {/* Search skeleton */}
   131→              <div className={cn('h-8 w-8 rounded animate-pulse', skeletonBg)} />
   132→            </div>
   133→            
   134→            {/* Right side - Media Type Filter skeleton */}
   135→            <div className="flex items-center gap-x-1.5">
   136→              <div className={cn('h-4 w-8 rounded animate-pulse', skeletonBg)} />
   137→              <div className={cn('h-8 w-[100px] rounded animate-pulse', skeletonBg)} />
   138→            </div>
   139→          </div>
   140→        </div>
   141→      )}
   142→      
   143→      {/* Gallery grid skeleton */}
   144→      <div
   145→        className={fixedColumns ? cn('grid', gapClasses) : gridCols}
   146→        style={fixedColumns ? { gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${fixedColumns}, minmax(0, 1fr))` } : undefined}
   147→      >
   148→        {Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, idx) => (
   149→          <div
   150→            key={idx}
   151→            className={cn(
   152→              'animate-pulse rounded-lg relative',
   153→              skeletonBg
   154→            )}
   155→            style={{ paddingBottom: aspectRatioPadding }}
   156→          />
   157→        ))}
   158→      </div>
   159→    </div>
   160→  );
   161→}
   162→
   163→export default React.memo(SkeletonGallery);

1→import {
     2→  DEFAULT_ROWS_PER_PAGE,
     3→  GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES,
     4→  ROW_LIMITS,
     5→  SKELETON_COLUMNS,
     6→  TARGET_IMAGE_WIDTH,
     7→} from './mediaGallery-constants';
     8→
     9→function parseAspectRatio(aspectRatioStr: string | undefined | null): number {
    10→  if (!aspectRatioStr) {
    11→    return 16 / 9;
    12→  }
    13→
    14→  const parts = aspectRatioStr.split(':');
    15→  if (parts.length !== 2) {
    16→    return 16 / 9;
    17→  }
    18→
    19→  const width = parseFloat(parts[0]);
    20→  const height = parseFloat(parts[1]);
    21→  if (Number.isNaN(width) || Number.isNaN(height) || height === 0) {
    22→    return 16 / 9;
    23→  }
    24→
    25→  return width / height;
    26→}
    27→
    28→function getEffectiveGap(reducedSpacing: boolean): number {
    29→  if (!reducedSpacing) {
    30→    return 16;
    31→  }
    32→
    33→  const isSmOrAbove = typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.innerWidth >= 640;
    34→  return isSmOrAbove ? 16 : 8;
    35→}
    36→
    37→function getColumnsForAspectRatio(aspectRatio: number): number {
    38→  const baseColumns = 5;
    39→  const computed = Math.round(baseColumns / Math.sqrt(aspectRatio)) + 2;
    40→  return Math.max(5, Math.min(9, computed));
    41→}
    42→
    43→function getTargetImageWidth(): number {
    44→  return TARGET_IMAGE_WIDTH.BASE;
    45→}
    46→
    47→function calculateDynamicColumns(
    48→  containerWidth: number,
    49→  aspectRatio: number,
    50→  gap: number = 16
    51→): number {
    52→  if (!containerWidth || containerWidth <= 0) {
    53→    return getColumnsForAspectRatio(aspectRatio);
    54→  }
    55→
    56→  const targetWidth = getTargetImageWidth();
    57→  const effectiveWidth = targetWidth + gap;
    58→  const columns = Math.floor((containerWidth + gap) / effectiveWidth);
    59→  return Math.max(
    60→    TARGET_IMAGE_WIDTH.MIN_COLUMNS,
    61→    Math.min(TARGET_IMAGE_WIDTH.MAX_COLUMNS, columns)
    62→  );
    63→}
    64→
    65→function calculateDynamicRows(
    66→  containerWidth: number,
    67→  containerHeight: number,
    68→  columns: number,
    69→  aspectRatio: number,
    70→  gap: number = 16
    71→): number {
    72→  if (!containerWidth || containerWidth <= 0 || !containerHeight || containerHeight <= 0) {
    73→    return DEFAULT_ROWS_PER_PAGE.DESKTOP;
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  const totalGapWidth = (columns - 1) * gap;
    77→  const imageWidth = (containerWidth - totalGapWidth) / columns;
    78→  const imageHeight = imageWidth / aspectRatio;
    79→  const effectiveHeight = imageHeight + gap;
    80→  const usableHeight = containerHeight - imageHeight * 0.5;
    81→  const rows = Math.floor((usableHeight + gap) / effectiveHeight);
    82→
    83→  return Math.max(
    84→    ROW_LIMITS.MIN_ROWS,
    85→    Math.min(ROW_LIMITS.MAX_ROWS, rows)
    86→  );
    87→}
    88→
    89→export function calculateGalleryLayout(
    90→  aspectRatioStr: string | undefined | null,
    91→  isMobile: boolean,
    92→  containerWidth?: number,
    93→  containerHeight?: number,
    94→  reducedSpacing: boolean = false
    95→): {
    96→  columns: number;
    97→  rows: number;
    98→  itemsPerPage: number;
    99→  skeletonColumns: typeof SKELETON_COLUMNS[keyof typeof SKELETON_COLUMNS];
   100→  gridColumnClasses: string;
   101→} {
   102→  const ratio = parseAspectRatio(aspectRatioStr);
   103→  const gap = getEffectiveGap(reducedSpacing);
   104→
   105→  const rawColumns = containerWidth && containerWidth > 0
   106→    ? calculateDynamicColumns(containerWidth, ratio, gap)
   107→    : getColumnsForAspectRatio(ratio);
   108→
   109→  const clampedColumns = Math.max(3, Math.min(12, rawColumns)) as keyof typeof GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES;
   110→
   111→  const rows = (!isMobile && containerWidth && containerWidth > 0 && containerHeight && containerHeight > 0)
   112→    ? calculateDynamicRows(containerWidth, containerHeight, rawColumns, ratio, gap)
   113→    : (isMobile ? DEFAULT_ROWS_PER_PAGE.MOBILE : DEFAULT_ROWS_PER_PAGE.DESKTOP);
   114→
   115→  const itemsPerPage = rawColumns * rows;
   116→  const skeletonColumns = SKELETON_COLUMNS[clampedColumns] || SKELETON_COLUMNS[5];
   117→  const gridColumnClasses = GRID_COLUMN_CLASSES[clampedColumns];
   118→
   119→  return {
   120→    columns: rawColumns,
   121→    rows,
   122→    itemsPerPage,
   123→    skeletonColumns,
   124→    gridColumnClasses,
   125→  };
   126→}
   127→

1→/**
     2→ * ProgressiveLoadingManager
     3→ * 
     4→ * Dedicated component for handling progressive image loading.
     5→ * Clear separation from preloading and gallery rendering concerns.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import React from 'react';
     9→import { useProgressiveImageLoading } from '@/shared/hooks/useProgressiveImageLoading';
    10→
    11→interface ProgressiveLoadingManagerProps {
    12→  images: { id: string }[];
    13→  page: number;
    14→  enabled?: boolean;
    15→  isMobile: boolean;
    16→  onImagesReady?: () => void;
    17→  isLightboxOpen?: boolean;
    18→  instanceId?: string; // Unique ID to prevent state conflicts between multiple instances
    19→  children: (showImageIndices: Set<number>) => React.ReactNode;
    20→}
    21→
    22→/**
    23→ * This component manages progressive loading and provides the showImageIndices
    24→ * to its children via render prop pattern for clear data flow
    25→ */
    26→const ProgressiveLoadingManagerComponent: React.FC<ProgressiveLoadingManagerProps> = ({
    27→  images,
    28→  page,
    29→  enabled = true,
    30→  isMobile,
    31→  onImagesReady,
    32→  isLightboxOpen = false,
    33→  instanceId,
    34→  children
    35→}) => {
    36→  
    37→  const { showImageIndices } = useProgressiveImageLoading({
    38→    images,
    39→    page,
    40→    enabled,
    41→    isMobile,
    42→    onImagesReady,
    43→    isLightboxOpen,
    44→    instanceId,
    45→  });
    46→
    47→  // Render children with showImageIndices via render prop
    48→  return <>{children(showImageIndices)}</>;
    49→};
    50→
    51→// Memoize with custom comparison for performance
    52→export const ProgressiveLoadingManager = React.memo(ProgressiveLoadingManagerComponent, (prevProps, nextProps) => {
    53→  return (
    54→    prevProps.images === nextProps.images &&
    55→    prevProps.page === nextProps.page &&
    56→    prevProps.enabled === nextProps.enabled &&
    57→    prevProps.isMobile === nextProps.isMobile &&
    58→    prevProps.isLightboxOpen === nextProps.isLightboxOpen &&
    59→    prevProps.instanceId === nextProps.instanceId &&
    60→    prevProps.onImagesReady === nextProps.onImagesReady
    61→  );
    62→});
    63→

Perfect! Now let me create a comprehensive summary document with all the findings:

## Summary: Edit Images Tool Architecture & Image Rendering

Based on my thorough exploration of the Edit Images tool codebase, here's a complete understanding of how the layout, image rendering, sizing, and animations work:

---

### 1. **Main Page Layout for Edit Images Tool**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/edit-images/pages/EditImagesPage.tsx`

The layout has three states:

**A) Loading State (showSkeleton):**
- Displays a skeleton loader matching the final layout
- Uses project aspect ratio to size the skeleton
- On mobile: `45dvh` height with centered skeleton
- On desktop: `60%` width container with skeleton
- **CSS:** `rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-black` wrapper

**B) Selection State (No selectedMedia):**
- A two-column layout (or stacked on mobile):
  - **Left panel (60% on desktop, 30% on mobile):** Dark placeholder with upload instructions
  - **Right panel (40% on desktop, 70% on mobile):** MediaSelectionPanel component
- Container: `flex flex-col md:flex-row rounded-2xl overflow-hidden` with `height: 65vh` (desktop) or `60vh` (mobile)
- Right panel classes: `bg-background border-t md:border-t-0 md:border-l border-border overflow-hidden relative z-[60] flex flex-col w-full h-[70%] md:w-[40%] md:h-full`

**C) Editing State (selectedMedia present):**
- Full-screen editor via `InlineEditView` component
- Mobile: Flex column with `45dvh` canvas on top, sidebar below
- Desktop: 60% left canvas, 40% right sidebar layout

---

### 2. **Images/Videos Rendered in Selection Panel**

**Primary Component:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaSelectionPanel.tsx`

Flow:
1. `MediaSelectionPanel` is a wrapper that sets up the gallery
2. It calls `MediaGallery` component with configured options:
   - `mediaType="image"` for Edit Images tool
   - `enableSingleClick: true` (single-click selection)
   - `showDelete: false, showDownload: false, showShare: false, showEdit: false, showStar: false, showAddToShot: false` (actions disabled)
   - `hideBottomPagination: true` (cleaner UI)
   - `videosAsThumbnails: mediaType === 'video'`

3. `MediaGallery` renders individual items via `MediaGalleryItem` component

**MediaGalleryItem Structure** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem.tsx`):
```
<div>  // Outer container with border, rounded corners
  <div>  // Aspect ratio container with paddingBottom
    <div>  // Inner content area (image OR video OR error state)
      <ImageContent /> OR <VideoContent />
    </div>
  </div>
  {/* Action buttons, badges, etc - only minimal set in selection panel */}
</div>
```

---

### 3. **Skeleton/Placeholder Logic in Selection Panel Grid**

**SkeletonGallery Component:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/ui/skeleton-gallery.tsx`

Skeleton Properties:
- **Aspect ratio calculation:** Uses project aspect ratio with bounds (60% minimum, 200% maximum padding-bottom)
- **Count:** Matches `itemsPerPage` (15 items for MediaSelectionPanel)
- **Grid columns:** Responsive via Tailwind classes (grid-cols-2, sm:grid-cols-3, etc.)
- **Styling:** `animate-pulse rounded-lg` with `bg-muted` background
- **Container:** `space-y-6 pb-8`

**Backfill Skeletons in MediaGalleryGrid** (line 318-333):
When server pagination deletes items, skeletons appear at the end:
```javascript
{computedSkeletonCount > 0 && Array.from({ length: computedSkeletonCount }).map((_, idx) => (
  <div key={`skeleton-${idx}`} className="relative group">
    <div className="border rounded-lg overflow-hidden transition-all duration-200 relative bg-card">
      <div className="relative w-full">
        <div
          style={{ paddingBottom: skeletonAspectRatio, minHeight: '120px' }}
          className="relative bg-muted/50"
        >
          <div className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-muted/30 animate-pulse">
            <div className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin rounded-full border-b-2 border-muted-foreground"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
))}
```

**Full Page Loading:** When initially loading gallery, uses `SkeletonGallery` with `showControls={false}` (line 225-241 of MediaGalleryGrid)

---

### 4. **Image Sizing & Dimensions**

**Aspect Ratio System** (Primary method):

**Project Aspect Ratio Calculation:**
```javascript
// In MediaGalleryItem.tsx lines 316-353
const projectAspectRatio = selectedProject?.aspectRatio || '16:9';
let aspectRatioPadding = '100%'; // Default square

if (projectAspectRatio) {
  const ratio = parseRatio(projectAspectRatio);
  if (!isNaN(ratio)) {
    const calculatedPadding = (1 / ratio) * 100; // height/width * 100
    const minPadding = 60;   // Min 60% (for very wide: 16:9 → ~56%)
    const maxPadding = 200;  // Max 200% (for very tall: 9:16 → ~178%)
    aspectRatioPadding = `${Math.min(Math.max(calculatedPadding, minPadding), maxPadding)}%`;
  }
}
```

Applied via padding-bottom trick:
```jsx
<div
  style={{
    paddingBottom: aspectRatioPadding,  // e.g., "56.25%" for 16:9
    minHeight: minHeight  // '120px' minimum
  }}
  className="relative bg-muted/50"
>
```

**Grid Column Calculation** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/utils/mediaGallery-layout.ts`):
- **Base target image width:** 100px (`TARGET_IMAGE_WIDTH.BASE`)
- **Gap:** 16px (default) or 8px (reduced spacing)
- **Dynamic columns:** `Math.floor((containerWidth + gap) / (targetWidth + gap))`
- **Constrained:** 2-24 columns
- **For 16:9 aspect ratio:** ~5 columns on desktop, fewer on mobile

**Responsive Grid Classes** (from `mediaGallery-constants.ts`):
```javascript
5: 'grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-5'
```

---

### 5. **CSS Transitions & Animations**

**Main Image Transitions:**

In `ImageContent.tsx` (line 67):
```jsx
className={cn(
  "absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:opacity-80 transition-all duration-300",
  progressiveEnabled && isThumbShowing && "opacity-90",
  progressiveEnabled && isFullLoaded && "opacity-100"
)}
```
- **Transition:** `transition-all duration-300` (0.3s smooth opacity change)
- **Hover effect:** `group-hover:opacity-80` (80% opacity on hover)
- **Progressive loading:** Opacity ramps from thumbnail (90%) → full (100%)

**Container Hover Effects** (`MediaGalleryItem.tsx` line 429):
```jsx
className={`border rounded-lg overflow-hidden hover:shadow-md transition-all duration-200 relative group bg-card ${
  isDragging ? 'opacity-50 scale-75' : ''
}`}
```
- **Transition:** `transition-all duration-200` (0.2s)
- **Hover:** `hover:shadow-md` (subtle shadow on hover)
- **Drag state:** `scale-75 opacity-50` (shrinks to 75% when dragging)

**Action Buttons Fade-In:**
- **Share button:** `opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-all` (fades in on hover)
- **Shot navigation:** `opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100` (same fade-in)

**Loading States:**
- **Skeleton pulse:** `animate-pulse` (Tailwind's built-in pulse animation)
- **Spinner:** `animate-spin rounded-full border-b-2` (rotating border)
- **Loading overlay:** `bg-muted/30 animate-pulse pointer-events-none`

**No "grow" Animation:**
The images don't actually "grow" from small to full size in the current implementation. Instead:
1. Skeleton shows with correct aspect ratio
2. When image loads, it fills the container with `absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover`
3. The layout shift from skeleton to image is instantaneous (no scale/grow animation)
4. Progressive loading adds opacity transitions (thumbnail → full quality)

---

### 6. **Key Files Reference**

| Component | Path | Purpose |
|-----------|------|---------|
| Edit Images Page | `src/tools/edit-images/pages/EditImagesPage.tsx` | Main layout, selection/editing orchestration |
| Selection Panel | `src/shared/components/MediaSelectionPanel.tsx` | Right panel wrapper with gallery setup |
| Media Gallery | `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.tsx` | Full gallery with filtering, pagination |
| Gallery Grid | `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/components/MediaGalleryGrid.tsx` | Grid rendering with skeleton backfill |
| Gallery Item | `src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem.tsx` | Individual image card rendering |
| Image Content | `src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem/components/ImageContent.tsx` | Image display with progressive loading |
| Skeleton Gallery | `src/shared/components/ui/skeleton-gallery.tsx` | Reusable skeleton loader |
| Layout Utils | `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/utils/mediaGallery-layout.ts` | Aspect ratio & column calculations |
| Layout Constants | `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/utils/mediaGallery-constants.ts` | Grid column classes, target widths |

---

### 7. **Progressive Loading Flow**

The `ProgressiveLoadingManager` wraps the grid to progressively show images:
1. Initially, items outside viewport are hidden (`shouldLoad: false`)
2. As user scrolls, images become visible (`shouldLoad: true`)
3. Hidden `<img>` tag loads in background (`display: none`)
4. Once loaded, visible `<img>` tag appears with `transition-all duration-300`
5. For thumbnails, opacity fades from 90% → 100%

This prevents heavy initial load from loading all images at once.

    30→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string) => Promise<void>;
    31→}
    32→
    33→// ============================================================================
    34→// InlineEditCanvas — media display with all overlay controls
    35→// ============================================================================
    36→
    37→interface InlineEditCanvasProps {
    38→  variant: 'mobile' | 'desktop';
    39→  model: InlineEditCanvasModel;
    40→  media: GenerationRow;
    41→}
    42→
    43→interface InlineEditCanvasModel {
    44→  imageEditValue: InlineEditStateResult['imageEditValue'];
    45→  media: {
    46→    effectiveImageUrl: string;
    47→    isVideo: boolean;
    48→    imageDimensions: InlineEditStateResult['canvasEnvironment']['imageDimensions'];
    49→    setImageDimensions: InlineEditStateResult['canvasEnvironment']['setImageDimensions'];
    50→  };
    51→  annotation: {
    52→    selectedShapeId: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['selectedShapeId'];
    53→    isAnnotateMode: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['isAnnotateMode'];
    54→    brushStrokes: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['brushStrokes'];
    55→    getDeleteButtonPosition: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['getDeleteButtonPosition'];
    56→    handleToggleFreeForm: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['handleToggleFreeForm'];
    57→    handleDeleteSelected: InlineEditStateResult['inpaintingState']['handleDeleteSelected'];
    58→  };
    59→  generation: {
    60→    handleDownload: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleDownload'];
    61→    localStarred: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['localStarred'];
    62→    handleToggleStar: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleToggleStar'];
    63→    toggleStarPending: boolean;
    64→  };
    65→  isSpecialEditMode: boolean;
    66→}
    67→
    68→function buildInlineEditCanvasModel(state: InlineEditStateResult): InlineEditCanvasModel {
    69→  return {
    70→    imageEditValue: state.imageEditValue,
    71→    media: {
    72→      effectiveImageUrl: state.canvasEnvironment.effectiveImageUrl,
    73→      isVideo: state.canvasEnvironment.isVideo,
    74→      imageDimensions: state.canvasEnvironment.imageDimensions,
    75→      setImageDimensions: state.canvasEnvironment.setImageDimensions,
    76→    },
    77→    annotation: {
    78→      selectedShapeId: state.inpaintingState.selectedShapeId,
    79→      isAnnotateMode: state.inpaintingState.isAnnotateMode,
    80→      brushStrokes: state.inpaintingState.brushStrokes,
    81→      getDeleteButtonPosition: state.inpaintingState.getDeleteButtonPosition,
    82→      handleToggleFreeForm: state.inpaintingState.handleToggleFreeForm,
    83→      handleDeleteSelected: state.inpaintingState.handleDeleteSelected,
    84→    },
    85→    generation: {
    86→      handleDownload: state.generationState.handleDownload,
    87→      localStarred: state.generationState.localStarred,
    88→      handleToggleStar: state.generationState.handleToggleStar,
    89→      toggleStarPending: state.generationState.toggleStarMutation.isPending,
    90→    },
    91→    isSpecialEditMode: state.inpaintingState.isSpecialEditMode,
    92→  };
    93→}
    94→
    95→function InlineEditCanvas({ variant, model, media }: InlineEditCanvasProps) {
    96→  const isMobileVariant = variant === 'mobile';
    97→
    98→  return (
    99→    <ImageEditProvider value={model.imageEditValue}>
   100→      <MediaDisplayWithCanvas
   101→        effectiveImageUrl={model.media.effectiveImageUrl}
   102→        thumbUrl={media.thumbnail_url || media.thumbUrl}
   103→        isVideo={model.media.isVideo}
   104→        onImageLoad={model.media.setImageDimensions}
   105→        variant={isMobileVariant ? "mobile-stacked" : "desktop-side-panel"}
   106→        containerClassName={isMobileVariant ? "w-full h-full" : "max-w-full max-h-full"}
   107→        debugContext={isMobileVariant ? "Mobile Inline" : "InlineEdit"}
   108→        imageDimensions={model.media.imageDimensions}
   109→      />
   110→
   111→      {!isMobileVariant && (
   112→        <AnnotationFloatingControls
   113→          selectedShapeId={model.annotation.selectedShapeId}
   114→          isAnnotateMode={model.annotation.isAnnotateMode}
   115→          brushStrokes={model.annotation.brushStrokes}
   116→          getDeleteButtonPosition={model.annotation.getDeleteButtonPosition}
   117→          onToggleFreeForm={model.annotation.handleToggleFreeForm}
   118→          onDeleteSelected={model.annotation.handleDeleteSelected}
   119→          positionStrategy="absolute"
   120→        />
   121→      )}
   122→
   123→      {model.isSpecialEditMode && (
   124→        <FloatingToolControls
   125→          variant={isMobileVariant ? "mobile" : "tablet"}
   126→        />
   127→      )}
   128→
   129→      <TopRightControls
   130→        showDownload={true}
   131→        handleDownload={model.generation.handleDownload}
   132→      />
   133→
   134→      <BottomLeftControls
   135→        localStarred={model.generation.localStarred}
   136→        handleToggleStar={model.generation.handleToggleStar}
   137→        toggleStarPending={model.generation.toggleStarPending}
   138→      />
   139→
   140→      <BottomRightControls
   141→        showAddToReferences={false}
   142→      />
   143→    </ImageEditProvider>
   144→  );
   145→}
   146→
   147→// ============================================================================
   148→// InlineEditSidebar — EditModePanel or fallback placeholder
   149→// ============================================================================
   150→
   151→interface InlineEditSidebarProps {
   152→  variant: 'mobile' | 'desktop';
   153→  model: InlineEditSidebarModel;
   154→  onClose: () => void;
   155→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string) => Promise<void>;
   156→}
   157→
   158→interface InlineEditSidebarModel {
   159→  isSpecialEditMode: boolean;
   160→  sourceGenerationData: InlineEditStateResult['sourceGenerationData'];
   161→  currentMediaId: string;
   162→  generation: {
   163→    handleUnifiedGenerate: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleUnifiedGenerate'];
   164→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit'];
   165→    handleGenerateReposition: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleGenerateReposition'];
   166→    handleSaveAsVariant: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleSaveAsVariant'];
   167→    handleGenerateImg2Img: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['handleGenerateImg2Img'];
   168→    img2imgLoraManager: InlineEditStateResult['generationState']['img2imgLoraManager'];
   169→  };
   170→  availableLoras: InlineEditStateResult['availableLoras'];
   171→  imageEditValue: InlineEditStateResult['imageEditValue'];
   172→  variants: {
   173→    list: InlineEditStateResult['variants']['variants'];
   174→    activeVariantId: string | null;
   175→    isLoading: boolean;
   176→    setActiveVariantId: InlineEditStateResult['variants']['setActiveVariantId'];
   177→    setPrimaryVariant: InlineEditStateResult['variants']['setPrimaryVariant'];
   178→    deleteVariant: InlineEditStateResult['variants']['deleteVariant'];
   179→  };
   180→  actions: {
   181→    enterInpaintMode: () => void;
   182→    enterMagicEditMode: () => void;
   183→  };
   184→}
   185→
   186→function buildInlineEditSidebarModel(state: InlineEditStateResult): InlineEditSidebarModel {
   187→  return {
   188→    isSpecialEditMode: state.inpaintingState.isSpecialEditMode,
   189→    sourceGenerationData: state.sourceGenerationData,
   190→    currentMediaId: state.media.id,
   191→    generation: {
   192→      handleUnifiedGenerate: state.generationState.handleUnifiedGenerate,
   193→      handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit: state.generationState.handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit,
   194→      handleGenerateReposition: state.generationState.handleGenerateReposition,
   195→      handleSaveAsVariant: state.generationState.handleSaveAsVariant,
   196→      handleGenerateImg2Img: state.generationState.handleGenerateImg2Img,
   197→      img2imgLoraManager: state.generationState.img2imgLoraManager,
   198→    },
   199→    availableLoras: state.availableLoras,
   200→    imageEditValue: state.imageEditValue,
   201→    variants: {
   202→      list: state.variants.variants,
   203→      activeVariantId: state.variants.activeVariant?.id || null,
   204→      isLoading: state.variants.isLoading,
   205→      setActiveVariantId: state.variants.setActiveVariantId,
   206→      setPrimaryVariant: state.variants.setPrimaryVariant,
   207→      deleteVariant: state.variants.deleteVariant,
   208→    },
   209→    actions: {
   210→      enterInpaintMode: () => {
   211→        state.inpaintingState.setIsInpaintMode(true);
   212→        state.inpaintingState.setEditMode('inpaint');
   213→      },
   214→      enterMagicEditMode: state.inpaintingState.handleEnterMagicEditMode,
   215→    },
   216→  };
   217→}
   218→
   219→function InlineEditSidebar({ variant, model, onClose, onNavigateToGeneration }: InlineEditSidebarProps) {
   220→  const sidebarContent = model.isSpecialEditMode ? (
   221→    <EditModePanel
   222→      variant={variant === 'mobile' ? 'mobile' : 'desktop'}
   223→      hideInfoEditToggle={true}
   224→      simplifiedHeader={true}
   225→      sourceGenerationData={model.sourceGenerationData}
   226→      onOpenExternalGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration ?
   227→        async (id) => onNavigateToGeneration(id) : undefined
   228→      }
   229→      currentMediaId={model.currentMediaId}
   230→      handleUnifiedGenerate={model.generation.handleUnifiedGenerate}
   231→      handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit={model.generation.handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit}
   232→      handleGenerateReposition={model.generation.handleGenerateReposition}
   233→      handleSaveAsVariant={model.generation.handleSaveAsVariant}
   234→      handleGenerateImg2Img={model.generation.handleGenerateImg2Img}
   235→      img2imgLoraManager={model.generation.img2imgLoraManager}
   236→      availableLoras={model.availableLoras}
   237→      coreState={{ onClose }}
   238→      imageEditState={model.imageEditValue}
   239→    />
   240→  ) : (
   241→    <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center gap-y-4">
   242→      <h3 className="text-xl font-medium">Image Editor</h3>
   243→      <p className="text-muted-foreground">Select an option to start editing</p>
   244→
   245→      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 w-full max-w-xs">
   246→        <Button onClick={() => {
   247→          model.actions.enterInpaintMode();
   248→        }} className="w-full">
   249→          Inpaint / Erase
   250→        </Button>
   251→
   252→        <Button onClick={model.actions.enterMagicEditMode} variant="secondary" className="w-full">
   253→          Magic Edit
   254→        </Button>
   255→      </div>
   256→    </div>
   257→  );
   258→
   259→  return (
   260→    <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col min-h-0">
   261→      <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto">
   262→        {sidebarContent}
   263→      </div>
   264→      <div
   265→        className={cn(
   266→          'border-t border-border bg-background/95 backdrop-blur-sm px-3 pt-3 pb-6',
   267→          variant === 'mobile' ? 'pb-8' : 'pb-6'
   268→        )}
   269→      >
   270→        <VariantSelector
   271→          variants={model.variants.list}
   272→          activeVariantId={model.variants.activeVariantId}
   273→          onVariantSelect={(variantId) => model.variants.setActiveVariantId(variantId)}
   274→          onMakePrimary={model.variants.setPrimaryVariant}
   275→          isLoading={model.variants.isLoading}
   276→          onDeleteVariant={model.variants.deleteVariant}
   277→        />
   278→      </div>
   279→    </div>
   280→  );
   281→}
   282→
   283→// ============================================================================
   284→// InlineEditView — layout orchestrator (mobile vs desktop)
   285→// ============================================================================
   286→
   287→export function InlineEditView({ media, onClose, onNavigateToGeneration }: InlineEditViewProps) {
   288→  const state = useInlineEditState(media, onNavigateToGeneration);
   289→  const canvasModel = buildInlineEditCanvasModel(state);
   290→  const sidebarModel = buildInlineEditSidebarModel(state);
   291→  const isMobile = state.canvasEnvironment.isMobile;
   292→
   293→  if (isMobile) {
   294→    return (
   295→      <TooltipProvider delayDuration={500}>
   296→        <div className="w-full flex flex-col bg-transparent">
   297→          <div
   298→              className="flex items-center justify-center relative bg-black w-full shrink-0 rounded-t-2xl overflow-hidden"
   299→              style={{ height: '45dvh', touchAction: 'pan-y' }}
   300→            >
   301→              <InlineEditCanvas variant="mobile" model={canvasModel} media={media} />
   302→            </div>
   303→
   304→            <div
   305→              className={cn(
   306→                "bg-background border-t border-border relative z-[60] w-full rounded-b-2xl pb-8"
   307→              )}
   308→              style={{ minHeight: '55dvh' }}
   309→            >
   310→              <InlineEditSidebar
   311→                variant="mobile"
   312→                model={sidebarModel}
   313→                onClose={onClose}
   314→                onNavigateToGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration}
   315→              />
   316→            </div>
   317→          </div>
   318→        </TooltipProvider>
   319→    );
   320→  }
   321→
   322→  return (
   323→    <TooltipProvider delayDuration={500}>
   324→      <div className="w-full h-full flex bg-transparent overflow-hidden">
   325→        <div
   326→            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center relative bg-black rounded-l-xl overflow-hidden"
   327→            style={{ width: '60%', height: '100%' }}
   328→          >
   329→            <InlineEditCanvas variant="desktop" model={canvasModel} media={media} />
   330→          </div>
   331→
   332→          <div
   333→            className={cn(
   334→              "bg-background border-l border-border overflow-y-auto relative z-[60] rounded-r-xl"
   335→            )}
   336→            style={{ width: '40%', height: '100%' }}
   337→          >
   338→            <InlineEditSidebar
   339→              variant="desktop"
   340→              model={sidebarModel}
   341→              onClose={onClose}
   342→              onNavigateToGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration}
   343→            />
   344→          </div>
   345→        </div>
   346→      </TooltipProvider>
   347→  );
   348→}

   385→    setEnablePromptExpansion: img2img.setEnablePromptExpansion,

   407→
   408→  return {

   416→export interface InlineEditStateResult {
   417→  media: GenerationRow;
   418→  canvasEnvironment: {

   430→  inpaintingState: {

   451→    setIsInpaintMode: InpaintingHookResult['setIsInpaintMode'];
   452→    setEditMode: InpaintingHookResult['setEditMode'];
   453→    setBrushSize: InpaintingHookResult['setBrushSize'];
   454→    setIsEraseMode: InpaintingHookResult['setIsEraseMode'];
   455→    setAnnotationMode: InpaintingHookResult['setAnnotationMode'];
   456→    isSpecialEditMode: boolean;
   457→    handleEnterMagicEditMode: MagicEditHookResult['handleEnterMagicEditMode'];
   458→  };
   459→  transformState: {
   460→    repositionTransform: RepositionHookResult['transform'];
   461→    setScale: RepositionHookResult['setScale'];
   462→    setRotation: RepositionHookResult['setRotation'];
   463→    toggleFlipH: RepositionHookResult['toggleFlipH'];
   464→    toggleFlipV: RepositionHookResult['toggleFlipV'];
   465→    resetTransform: RepositionHookResult['resetTransform'];
   466→    getTransformStyle: RepositionHookResult['getTransformStyle'];
   467→  };
   468→  generationState: {
   469→    localStarred: StarToggleHookResult['localStarred'];
   470→    toggleStarMutation: StarToggleHookResult['toggleStarMutation'];
   471→    handleToggleStar: StarToggleHookResult['handleToggleStar'];
   472→    handleDownload: () => Promise<void>;
   473→    handleUnifiedGenerate: MagicEditHookResult['handleUnifiedGenerate'];
   474→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit: InpaintingHookResult['handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit'];
   475→    handleGenerateReposition: RepositionHookResult['handleGenerateReposition'];
   476→    handleSaveAsVariant: RepositionHookResult['handleSaveAsVariant'];
   477→    handleGenerateImg2Img: Img2ImgHookResult['handleGenerateImg2Img'];
   478→    img2imgLoraManager: Img2ImgHookResult['loraManager'];
   479→  };
   480→  variants: {
   481→    variants: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['variants'];
   482→    activeVariant: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['activeVariant'];
   483→    isLoading: boolean;
   484→    setActiveVariantId: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['setActiveVariantId'];
   485→    setPrimaryVariant: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['setPrimaryVariant'];
   486→    deleteVariant: InlineEditPersistence['variants']['deleteVariant'];
   487→  };
   488→  sourceGenerationData: SourceGenerationData;
   489→  availableLoras: PublicLorasData;

   492→
   493→export function useInlineEditState(
   494→  media: GenerationRow,
   495→  onNavigateToGeneration?: (generationId: string) => Promise<void>,
   496→): InlineEditStateResult {
   497→  const env = useInlineEditEnvironment(media);
   498→  const persistence = useInlineEditPersistence(env.actualGenerationId, env.selectedProjectId);
   499→  const activeVariant = persistence.variants.activeVariant;
   500→  const effectiveImageUrl = activeVariant?.location || env.effectiveImageUrl;
   501→
   502→  useEffect(() => {
   503→    env.setUpscaleActiveVariant(activeVariant?.location, activeVariant?.id);
   504→  }, [env, activeVariant?.location, activeVariant?.id]);
   505→
   506→  const { data: availableLoras } = usePublicLoras();
   507→  const { inpainting, magic } = useInlineEditInpaintingAndMagic(media, env, persistence);
   508→  const reposition = useInlineEditReposition(media, env, inpainting, magic, persistence);
   509→  const img2img = useInlineEditImg2Img(media, env, persistence, availableLoras);
   510→
   511→  useInlineEditSettingsSync(env.actualGenerationId, persistence.editSettings, inpainting);
   512→
   513→  const { sourceGenerationData } = useSourceGeneration({
   514→    media,
   515→    onOpenExternalGeneration: onNavigateToGeneration
   516→      ? async (id) => onNavigateToGeneration(id)
   517→      : undefined,
   518→  });
   519→
   520→  const starToggle = useStarToggle({ media });
   521→
   522→  const handleDownload = async () => {
   523→    await downloadMedia(effectiveImageUrl, media.id, env.isVideo, media.contentType);
   524→  };
   525→
   526→  const {
   527→    inpaintingState,
   528→    transformState,
   529→    generationState,
   530→    imageEditValue,
   531→  } = buildInlineEditStateModels({
   532→    env,
   533→    persistence,
   534→    inpainting,
   535→    magic,
   536→    reposition,
   537→    img2img,
   538→    starToggle,
   539→    handleDownload,
   540→  });
   541→
   542→  return {
   543→    media,
   544→    canvasEnvironment: {
   545→      isMobile: env.isMobile,
   546→      selectedProjectId: env.selectedProjectId,
   547→      isCloudMode: env.isCloudMode,
   548→      isVideo: env.isVideo,
   549→      imageContainerRef: env.imageContainerRef,
   550→      effectiveImageUrl,
   551→      imageDimensions: env.imageDimensions,
   552→      setImageDimensions: env.setImageDimensions,
   553→      isUpscaling: env.isUpscaling,
   554→      handleUpscale: env.handleUpscale,
   555→    },
   556→    inpaintingState,
   557→    transformState,
   558→    generationState,
   559→    variants: {
   560→      variants: persistence.variants.variants,
   561→      activeVariant: persistence.variants.activeVariant,
   562→      isLoading: persistence.variants.isLoading,
   563→      setActiveVariantId: persistence.variants.setActiveVariantId,
   564→      setPrimaryVariant: persistence.variants.setPrimaryVariant,
   565→      deleteVariant: persistence.variants.deleteVariant,
   566→    },
   567→    sourceGenerationData,
   568→    availableLoras,
   569→    imageEditValue,
   570→  } satisfies InlineEditStateResult;
   571→}
   572→
```

    99→  // Computed values
   100→  const isAnnotateMode = editMode === 'annotate';
   101→  const [isEraseMode, setIsEraseMode] = useState(false);
   102→
   103→  // Computed: current brush strokes based on mode
   104→  const brushStrokes = useMemo(() => {
   105→    return editMode === 'annotate' ? annotationStrokes : editMode === 'inpaint' ? inpaintStrokes : [];
   106→  }, [editMode, annotationStrokes, inpaintStrokes]);
   107→
   108→  // ============================================
   109→  // StrokeOverlay callbacks
   110→  // ============================================
   111→
   112→  /**
   113→   * Called when a new stroke is completed (drawn and released).
   114→   * Appends to the correct stroke array based on current mode.
   115→   */
   116→  const onStrokeComplete = useCallback((stroke: BrushStroke) => {
   117→    if (isAnnotateMode) {
   118→      // In annotate+rectangle mode, limit to one rectangle
   119→      if (stroke.shapeType === 'rectangle' && annotationStrokes.length > 0) {
   120→        setAnnotationStrokes([stroke]);
   121→      } else {
   122→        setAnnotationStrokes(prev => [...prev, stroke]);
   123→      }
   124→    } else {
   125→      setInpaintStrokes(prev => [...prev, stroke]);
   126→    }
   127→  }, [isAnnotateMode, annotationStrokes.length, setAnnotationStrokes, setInpaintStrokes]);
   128→
   129→  /**
   130→   * Called when strokes are mutated in place (drag/resize/free-form).
   131→   * Replaces the entire stroke array for the current mode.
   132→   */
   133→  const onStrokesChange = useCallback((newStrokes: BrushStroke[]) => {
   134→    if (isAnnotateMode) {
   135→      setAnnotationStrokes(newStrokes);
   136→    } else {
   137→      setInpaintStrokes(newStrokes);
   138→    }
   139→  }, [isAnnotateMode, setAnnotationStrokes, setInpaintStrokes]);
   140→
   141→  /**
   142→   * Called when selection changes inside StrokeOverlay.
   143→   */
   144→  const onSelectionChange = useCallback((shapeId: string | null) => {
   145→    setSelectedShapeId(shapeId);
   146→  }, []);
   147→
   148→  /**
   149→   * Called when user tries to draw in text mode.
   150→   */
   151→  const onTextModeHint = useCallback(() => {
   152→    setShowTextModeHint(true);
   153→    setTimeout(() => setShowTextModeHint(false), 2000);
   154→  }, []);
   155→
   156→  // ============================================
   157→  // User Actions (delegate to StrokeOverlay ref)
   158→  // ============================================
   159→  const handleUndo = useCallback(() => {
   160→    strokeOverlayRef.current?.undo();
   161→  }, []);
   162→
   163→  const handleClearMask = useCallback(() => {
   164→    strokeOverlayRef.current?.clear();
   165→  }, []);
   166→
   167→  const handleDeleteSelected = useCallback(() => {

   169→  }, []);
   170→
   171→  const handleToggleFreeForm = useCallback(() => {
   172→    strokeOverlayRef.current?.toggleFreeForm();
   173→  }, []);
   174→
   175→  // ============================================
   176→  // Task Generation
   177→  // ============================================
   178→  const {
   179→    handleGenerateInpaint,
   180→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit,
   181→  } = useTaskGeneration({

    13→// Re-export types for backward compatibility
    14→export type { ImageTransform, UseRepositionModeProps, UseRepositionModeReturn };
    15→
    16→/**
    17→ * Hook for managing image reposition mode.
    18→ * Orchestrates transform state, drag interaction, mask generation, and task/variant creation.
    19→ *
    20→ * This is a composition of focused hooks:
    21→ * - useImageTransform: Transform state with per-variant caching
    22→ * - useCanvasTransform: CSS transform preview and canvas manipulation
    23→ * - useRepositionDrag: Drag-to-move pointer event handling
    24→ * - useRepositionTaskCreation: Inpaint task creation with mask generation
    25→ * - useRepositionVariantSave: Save as variant/generation with thumbnail
    26→ */
    27→export const useRepositionMode = ({
    28→  media,
    29→  selectedProjectId,
    30→  shotId,
    31→  toolTypeOverride,
    32→  imageDimensions,
    33→  imageContainerRef,
    34→  loras,
    35→  inpaintPrompt,
    36→  inpaintNumGenerations,

    42→  activeVariantId,
    43→  activeVariantParams,
    44→  qwenEditModel,
    45→}: UseRepositionModeProps): UseRepositionModeReturn => {
    46→  // Transform state management with per-variant caching
    47→  const transformHook = useImageTransform({
    48→    activeVariantId,
    49→    mediaId: media.id,
    50→    activeVariantParams,
    51→  });
    52→
    53→  const {
    54→    transform,
    55→    hasTransformChanges,
    56→    setTranslateX,
    57→    setTranslateY,
    58→    setScale,
    59→    setRotation,
    60→    toggleFlipH,
    61→    toggleFlipV,
    62→    resetTransform,
    63→    getCacheKey,
    64→    clearCacheForKey,
    65→    markSkipNextCache,
    66→  } = transformHook;
    67→
    68→  // Canvas transform for preview and actual transformation
    69→  const { getTransformStyle, createTransformedCanvas } = useCanvasTransform({
    70→    transform,
    71→    imageDimensions,
    72→    media,
    73→    activeVariantLocation,
    74→  });
    75→
    76→  // Drag handler for transform updates
    77→  const onTransformChange = useCallback((updates: Partial<ImageTransform>) => {
    78→    if (updates.translateX !== undefined) setTranslateX(updates.translateX);
    79→    if (updates.translateY !== undefined) setTranslateY(updates.translateY);
    80→  }, [setTranslateX, setTranslateY]);
    81→
    82→  // Drag-to-move functionality
    83→  const { isDragging, dragHandlers } = useRepositionDrag({
    84→    transform,
    85→    imageDimensions,
    86→    imageContainerRef,
    87→    onTransformChange,
    88→  });

1→import { useState, useCallback, useRef, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
     2→import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
     3→import { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';

     9→
    10→interface UseImg2ImgModeProps {
    11→  media: GenerationRow;
    12→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    13→  isVideo: boolean;
    14→  sourceUrlForTasks: string;
    15→  toolTypeOverride?: string;
    16→  createAsGeneration?: boolean;
    17→  availableLoras?: LoraModel[];
    18→  // Persisted values from editSettingsPersistence
    19→  img2imgStrength: number;
    20→  setImg2imgStrength: (strength: number) => void;
    21→  enablePromptExpansion: boolean;
    22→  setEnablePromptExpansion: (enabled: boolean) => void;
    23→  // Prompt is persisted per-generation (shared with other edit modes)
    24→  img2imgPrompt: string;
    25→  setImg2imgPrompt: (prompt: string) => void;
    26→  img2imgPromptHasBeenSet: boolean;
    27→  // Number of generations (shared with other edit modes)
    28→  numGenerations: number;
    29→  // Active variant's image URL - use this instead of sourceUrlForTasks when editing a variant
    30→  activeVariantLocation?: string | null;
    31→  // Active variant ID - for tracking source_variant_id in task params
    32→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    33→  // Shot ID for associating task with shot (enables navigation from TasksPane)
    34→  shotId?: string;
    35→}
    36→
    37→interface UseImg2ImgModeReturn {
    38→  // State
    39→  img2imgPrompt: string;
    40→  img2imgStrength: number;
    41→  enablePromptExpansion: boolean;
    42→  isGeneratingImg2Img: boolean;
    43→  img2imgGenerateSuccess: boolean;
    44→
    45→  // Setters
    46→  setImg2imgPrompt: (prompt: string) => void;
    47→  setImg2imgStrength: (strength: number) => void;
    48→  setEnablePromptExpansion: (enabled: boolean) => void;
    49→
    50→  // LoRA Manager (full access)
    51→  loraManager: UseLoraManagerReturn;
    52→
    53→  // Actions

    56→
    57→/**
    58→ * Hook for managing Img2Img mode state and generation
    59→ * Uses Z Image Turbo I2I task type with full LoRA selector support
    60→ * 
    61→ * Strength, enablePromptExpansion, and prompt are persisted via editSettingsPersistence
    62→ * Prompt is persisted per-generation (shared with other edit modes, never inherited)
    63→ */
    64→export const useImg2ImgMode = ({
    65→  media,
    66→  selectedProjectId,
    67→  isVideo,
    68→  sourceUrlForTasks,
    69→  toolTypeOverride,
    70→  createAsGeneration,
    71→  availableLoras = [],
    72→  // Persisted values
    73→  img2imgStrength,
    74→  setImg2imgStrength,
    75→  enablePromptExpansion,
    76→  setEnablePromptExpansion,
    77→  // Prompt is persisted per-generation
    78→  img2imgPrompt,
    79→  setImg2imgPrompt,
    80→  img2imgPromptHasBeenSet,
    81→  // Number of generations (shared)
    82→  numGenerations,
    83→  // Active variant location
    84→  activeVariantLocation,
    85→  // Active variant ID for tracking source
    86→  activeVariantId,
    87→  // Shot association
    88→  shotId,
    89→}: UseImg2ImgModeProps): UseImg2ImgModeReturn => {
    90→  // Local state (not persisted)
    91→  const [isGeneratingImg2Img, setIsGeneratingImg2Img] = useState(false);
    92→  const [img2imgGenerateSuccess, setImg2imgGenerateSuccess] = useState(false);
    93→  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
    94→
    95→  // Derive the base generation prompt from the generation params (best-effort)
    96→  const baseGenerationPrompt = useMemo(() => {
    97→    const params = media.params;
    98→    const raw =
    99→      (typeof params?.base_prompt === 'string' && params.base_prompt) ||
   100→      (typeof params?.prompt === 'string' && params.prompt) ||
   101→      '';
   102→    return raw.trim();
   103→  }, [media]);
   104→
   105→  // Default Img2Img prompt to the prompt that generated the base image (one-time per generation)
   106→  useEffect(() => {
   107→    if (img2imgPromptHasBeenSet) return;
   108→    if (img2imgPrompt && img2imgPrompt.trim().length > 0) return;
   109→    if (!baseGenerationPrompt) return;
   110→
   111→    setImg2imgPrompt(baseGenerationPrompt);
   112→  }, [img2imgPromptHasBeenSet, img2imgPrompt, baseGenerationPrompt, setImg2imgPrompt]);
   113→
   114→  // Use the shared LoRA manager hook
   115→  const loraManager = useLoraManager(availableLoras, {
   116→    projectId: selectedProjectId || undefined,
   117→    persistenceScope: 'none', // Don't persist for img2img - it's a quick tool
   118→    enableProjectPersistence: false,
   119→    disableAutoLoad: true,
   120→  });

     2→ * Handles task generation for inpainting and annotated edits.
     3→ * Exports mask from Konva, uploads to storage, and creates tasks.

     6→import { useCallback } from 'react';

    31→}
    32→
    33→const TASK_CONFIGS: Record<TaskType, TaskTypeConfig> = {
    34→  inpaint: {
    35→    logPrefix: '[Inpaint]',
    36→    emptyStrokesError: 'Please paint on the image first',
    37→    overlayNotReadyError: 'Paint overlay not ready',
    38→    fileNamePrefix: 'inpaint_mask',
    39→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create inpaint task',
    40→    createTask: createImageInpaintTask,
    41→  },

    46→    fileNamePrefix: 'annotated_edit_mask',
    47→    taskCreationError: 'Failed to create annotated edit task',
    48→    createTask: createAnnotatedImageEditTask,
    49→  },
    50→};
    51→
    52→interface UseTaskGenerationProps {
    53→  media: GenerationRow;
    54→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    55→  shotId?: string;
    56→  toolTypeOverride?: string;

    73→  setIsGeneratingInpaint: (isGenerating: boolean) => void;
    74→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess: (success: boolean) => void;
    75→}
    76→
    77→export function useTaskGeneration({
    78→  media,
    79→  selectedProjectId,
    80→  shotId,
    81→  toolTypeOverride,
    82→  isVideo,
    83→  loras,
    84→  activeVariantId,
    85→  activeVariantLocation,
    86→  createAsGeneration,
    87→  advancedSettings,
    88→  qwenEditModel,
    89→  inpaintStrokes,
    90→  annotationStrokes,
    91→  inpaintPrompt,
    92→  inpaintNumGenerations,
    93→  strokeOverlayRef,
    94→  handleExitInpaintMode,
    95→  setIsGeneratingInpaint,
    96→  setInpaintGenerateSuccess,
    97→}: UseTaskGenerationProps) {
    98→  // Get actual generation ID (may differ from media.id for shot_generations)
    99→  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   100→  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
   101→
   102→  /**
   103→   * Unified task generation function
   104→   * Handles both inpaint and annotate modes with mode-specific configuration
   105→   */
   106→  const handleGenerateTask = useCallback(async (taskType: TaskType) => {
   107→    const config = TASK_CONFIGS[taskType];
   108→    const strokes = taskType === 'inpaint' ? inpaintStrokes : annotationStrokes;
   109→
   110→    // Validation
   111→    if (!selectedProjectId || isVideo) {
   112→      toast.error(`Cannot generate ${taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'inpaint' : 'annotated edit'}`);
   113→      return;
   114→    }
   115→
   116→    if (strokes.length === 0) {
   117→      toast.error(config.emptyStrokesError);
   118→      return;
   119→    }
   120→
   121→    if (!inpaintPrompt.trim()) {
   122→      toast.error('Please enter a prompt');
   123→      return;
   124→    }
   125→
   126→    if (!strokeOverlayRef.current) {
   127→      toast.error(config.overlayNotReadyError);
   128→      return;
   129→    }
   130→
   131→    const incomingTaskType = taskType === 'inpaint' ? 'image_inpaint' : 'annotated_image_edit';
   132→
   133→    setIsGeneratingInpaint(true);
   134→    try {
   135→      await run({
   136→        taskType: incomingTaskType,
   137→        label: inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'Editing...',
   138→        context: 'useTaskGeneration',
   139→        toastTitle: config.taskCreationError,
   140→        create: async () => {
   141→          // Export mask directly from Konva
   142→          const maskImageData = strokeOverlayRef.current!.exportMask({ pixelRatio: 1.5 });
   143→
   144→          if (!maskImageData) {
   145→            throw new Error('Failed to export mask from overlay');
   146→          }
   147→
   148→          // Upload mask to storage
   149→          const maskFile = await fetch(maskImageData)
   150→            .then(res => res.blob())
   151→            .then(blob => new File([blob], `${config.fileNamePrefix}_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`, { type: 'image/png' }));
   152→
   153→          const maskUrl = await uploadImageToStorage(maskFile);
   154→
   155→          // Get source image URL
   156→          const mediaUrl = getMediaUrl(media) || media.imageUrl;
   157→          const sourceUrl = activeVariantLocation || mediaUrl;
   158→          if (!sourceUrl) {
   159→            throw new Error('Missing source media URL');
   160→          }
   161→          if (!actualGenerationId) {
   162→            throw new Error('Missing generation id');
   163→          }
   164→
   165→          return config.createTask({
   166→            project_id: selectedProjectId,
   167→            image_url: sourceUrl,
   168→            mask_url: maskUrl,
   169→            prompt: inpaintPrompt,
   170→            num_generations: inpaintNumGenerations,
   171→            generation_id: actualGenerationId ?? undefined,
   172→            shot_id: shotId,
   173→            tool_type: toolTypeOverride,
   174→            loras: loras,
   175→            create_as_generation: createAsGeneration,
   176→            source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
   177→            hires_fix: convertToHiresFixApiParams(advancedSettings),
   178→            qwen_edit_model: qwenEditModel,
   179→          });
   180→        },
   181→        onSuccess: () => {
   182→          // Show success state
   183→          setInpaintGenerateSuccess(true);
   184→
   185→          // Wait 1 second to show success, then exit
   186→          setTimeout(() => {
   187→            setInpaintGenerateSuccess(false);
   188→            handleExitInpaintMode();
   189→          }, 1000);
   190→        },
   191→      });
   192→    } finally {
   193→      setIsGeneratingInpaint(false);
   194→    }
   195→  }, [
   196→    selectedProjectId, isVideo, inpaintStrokes, annotationStrokes, inpaintPrompt, inpaintNumGenerations,
   197→    media, handleExitInpaintMode, shotId, toolTypeOverride, loras,
   198→    activeVariantLocation, activeVariantId, createAsGeneration, advancedSettings,
   199→    qwenEditModel, strokeOverlayRef, actualGenerationId,
   200→    setIsGeneratingInpaint, setInpaintGenerateSuccess, run
   201→  ]);
   202→
   203→  // Stable callbacks that preserve the original API
   204→  const handleGenerateInpaint = useCallback(() => {
   205→    return handleGenerateTask('inpaint');
   206→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   207→
   208→  const handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit = useCallback(() => {
   209→    return handleGenerateTask('annotate');
   210→  }, [handleGenerateTask]);
   211→
   212→  return {
   213→    handleGenerateInpaint,
   214→    handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit,
   215→  };
   216→}
   217→

1→import { createTask, processBatchResults, buildHiresFixParams } from '../taskCreation';
     2→import type { TaskCreationResult, HiresFixApiParams } from '../taskCreation';
     3→import type { ComfyLoraConfig } from '@/shared/types/lora';
     4→
     5→interface CreateImageInpaintTaskParams {
     6→  project_id: string;
     7→  image_url: string;
     8→  mask_url: string;
     9→  prompt: string;
    10→  num_generations: number;
    11→  generation_id?: string;
    12→  shot_id?: string;
    13→  tool_type?: string;
    14→  loras?: ComfyLoraConfig[];
    15→  create_as_generation?: boolean; // If true, create a new generation instead of a variant

    17→  // Advanced hires fix settings
    18→  hires_fix?: HiresFixApiParams;
    19→  // Qwen edit model selection (e.g., 'qwen-edit', 'qwen-edit-2509', 'qwen-edit-2511')
    20→  qwen_edit_model?: string;
    21→}
    22→
    23→/**
    24→ * Creates a single image inpainting task via the unified edge function
    25→ */
    26→function createSingleInpaintTask(params: Omit<CreateImageInpaintTaskParams, 'num_generations'>): Promise<TaskCreationResult> {
    27→  const {
    28→    project_id,
    29→    image_url,
    30→    mask_url,
    31→    prompt,
    32→    generation_id,
    33→    shot_id,
    34→    tool_type,
    35→    loras,
    36→    create_as_generation,
    37→    source_variant_id,
    38→    hires_fix,
    39→    qwen_edit_model,
    40→  } = params;
    41→
    42→  const taskParams = {
    43→    image_url,
    44→    mask_url,
    45→    prompt,
    46→    num_generations: 1, // Each task creates one generation
    47→    generation_id,
    48→    based_on: generation_id, // Explicitly track source generation for lineage
    49→    parent_generation_id: generation_id, // Compatibility for workers that key off parent_generation_id
    50→    ...(shot_id ? { shot_id } : {}),
    51→    ...(tool_type ? { tool_type } : {}),
    52→    ...(loras && loras.length > 0 ? { loras } : {}),
    53→    ...(create_as_generation ? { create_as_generation: true } : {}),
    54→    ...(source_variant_id ? { source_variant_id } : {}),
    55→    ...buildHiresFixParams(hires_fix),
    56→    ...(qwen_edit_model ? { qwen_edit_model } : {}),
    57→  };
    58→
    59→  return createTask({
    60→    project_id,
    61→    task_type: 'image_inpaint',
    62→    params: taskParams,
    63→  });
    64→}
    65→
    66→/**
    67→ * Creates image inpainting tasks
    68→ * Creates multiple tasks if num_generations > 1, using parallel execution
    69→ *
    70→ * @param params - Task parameters
    71→ * @returns First task ID (for backward compatibility)
    72→ */
    73→export async function createImageInpaintTask(params: CreateImageInpaintTaskParams): Promise<string> {
    74→  const { num_generations, ...singleTaskParams } = params;
    75→
    76→  // For single generation, create one task directly
    77→  if (num_generations === 1) {
    78→    const result = await createSingleInpaintTask(singleTaskParams);
    79→    return result.task_id;
    80→  }
    81→
    82→  // For multiple generations, create tasks in parallel
    83→  const results = await Promise.allSettled(
    84→    Array.from({ length: num_generations }, () => createSingleInpaintTask(singleTaskParams))
    85→  );
    86→
    87→  const successful = processBatchResults(results, 'ImageInpaint');
    88→  return successful[0].task_id; // Return first task ID for compatibility
    89→}
    90→

    24→/**
    25→ * Creates a single annotated image edit task via the unified edge function
    26→ */
    27→async function createSingleAnnotatedEditTask(params: Omit<CreateAnnotatedImageEditTaskParams, 'num_generations'>): Promise<string> {
    28→  const {
    29→    project_id,
    30→    image_url,
    31→    mask_url,
    32→    prompt,
    33→    generation_id,
    34→    shot_id,
    35→    tool_type,
    36→    loras,
    37→    create_as_generation,
    38→    source_variant_id,
    39→    hires_fix,
    40→    qwen_edit_model,
    41→  } = params;
    42→
    43→  const taskParams = {
    44→    image_url,
    45→    mask_url,
    46→    prompt,
    47→    num_generations: 1, // Each task creates one generation
    48→    generation_id,
    49→    based_on: generation_id, // Explicitly track source generation for lineage
    50→    parent_generation_id: generation_id, // Compatibility for workers that key off parent_generation_id
    51→    ...(shot_id ? { shot_id } : {}),
    52→    ...(tool_type ? { tool_type } : {}),
    53→    ...(loras && loras.length > 0 ? { loras } : {}),
    54→    ...(create_as_generation ? { create_as_generation: true } : {}),
    55→    ...(source_variant_id ? { source_variant_id } : {}),
    56→    ...buildHiresFixParams(hires_fix),
    57→    ...(qwen_edit_model ? { qwen_edit_model } : {}),
    58→  };
    59→
    60→  const result = await createTask({
    61→    project_id,
    62→    task_type: 'annotated_image_edit',
    63→    params: taskParams,
    64→  });
    65→
    66→  return result.task_id;
    67→}
    68→
    69→/**
    70→ * Creates annotated image edit tasks
    71→ * Creates multiple tasks if num_generations > 1, using parallel execution
    72→ * Similar to inpainting but for annotation-based edits
    73→ *
    74→ * @param params - Task parameters
    75→ * @returns First task ID (for backward compatibility)
    76→ */
    77→export async function createAnnotatedImageEditTask(params: CreateAnnotatedImageEditTaskParams): Promise<string> {
    78→  const { num_generations, ...singleTaskParams } = params;
    79→
    80→  // For single generation, create one task directly
    81→  if (num_generations === 1) {
    82→    const taskId = await createSingleAnnotatedEditTask(singleTaskParams);
    83→    return taskId;
    84→  }
    85→
    86→  // For multiple generations, create tasks in parallel
    87→
    88→  const results = await Promise.allSettled(
    89→    Array.from({ length: num_generations }, () => createSingleAnnotatedEditTask(singleTaskParams))
    90→  );
    91→
    92→  // Process results
    93→  const successful = results.filter((r): r is PromiseFulfilledResult<string> => r.status === 'fulfilled');
    94→  const failed = results.filter(r => r.status === 'rejected');
    95→
    96→  // If all failed, throw the first error
    97→  if (successful.length === 0) {
    98→    const firstError = results.find(r => r.status === 'rejected') as PromiseRejectedResult;
    99→    throw new Error(`All annotated image edit tasks failed: ${firstError.reason}`);
   100→  }
   101→
   102→  // If some failed, log warnings
   103→  if (failed.length > 0) {
   104→    failed.forEach((result, index) => {
   105→      if (result.status === 'rejected') {
   106→        normalizeAndPresentError(result.reason, { context: `AnnotatedImageEdit:task${index + 1}`, showToast: false });
   107→      }
   108→    });
   109→  }
   110→
   111→  const taskIds = successful.map(r => r.value);
   112→
   113→  return taskIds[0]; // Return first task ID for compatibility
   114→}
   115→

    11→import { useTaskPlaceholder } from '@/shared/hooks/useTaskPlaceholder';
    12→
    13→interface UseRepositionTaskCreationProps {
    14→  media: GenerationRow;
    15→  selectedProjectId: string | null;
    16→  shotId?: string;
    17→  toolTypeOverride?: string;
    18→  imageDimensions: { width: number; height: number } | null;
    19→  loras?: Array<{ url: string; strength: number }>;
    20→  inpaintPrompt: string;
    21→  inpaintNumGenerations: number;
    22→  transform: ImageTransform;
    23→  hasTransformChanges: boolean;
    24→  createAsGeneration?: boolean;
    25→  advancedSettings?: EditAdvancedSettings;
    26→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    27→  qwenEditModel?: QwenEditModel;
    28→  /** Function to create the transformed canvas */
    29→  createTransformedCanvas: () => Promise<HTMLCanvasElement>;
    30→}
    31→
    32→interface UseRepositionTaskCreationReturn {
    33→  isGeneratingReposition: boolean;
    34→  repositionGenerateSuccess: boolean;
    35→  handleGenerateReposition: () => Promise<void>;
    36→}
    37→
    38→/**
    39→ * Hook for generating inpaint tasks from repositioned images.
    40→ * Creates transformed image with mask for the dead space and submits to inpaint API.
    41→ */
    42→export function useRepositionTaskCreation({
    43→  media,
    44→  selectedProjectId,
    45→  shotId,
    46→  toolTypeOverride,
    47→  imageDimensions,
    48→  loras,
    49→  inpaintPrompt,
    50→  inpaintNumGenerations,
    51→  transform: _transform,
    52→  hasTransformChanges,
    53→  createAsGeneration,
    54→  advancedSettings,
    55→  activeVariantId,
    56→  qwenEditModel,
    57→  createTransformedCanvas,
    58→}: UseRepositionTaskCreationProps): UseRepositionTaskCreationReturn {
    59→  const [isGeneratingReposition, setIsGeneratingReposition] = useState(false);
    60→  const [repositionGenerateSuccess, setRepositionGenerateSuccess] = useState(false);
    61→  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
    62→
    63→  // Generate transformed image and mask, then create inpaint task
    64→  const handleGenerateReposition = useCallback(async () => {
    65→    if (!selectedProjectId || !imageDimensions) {
    66→      toast.error('Missing project or image dimensions');
    67→      return;
    68→    }
    69→
    70→    if (!hasTransformChanges) {
    71→      toast.error('Please move, scale, or rotate the image first');
    72→      return;
    73→    }
    74→
    75→    setIsGeneratingReposition(true);
    76→
    77→    try {
    78→      await run({
    79→        taskType: 'image_inpaint',
    80→        label: inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'Fill edges...',
    81→        context: 'useRepositionTaskCreation',
    82→        toastTitle: 'Failed to create reposition task',
    83→        create: async () => {
    84→          const transformedCanvas = await createTransformedCanvas();
    85→
    86→          // Generate mask from the transparent areas of the transformed image
    87→          const maskCanvas = generateMaskFromCanvas(transformedCanvas, {
    88→            alphaThreshold: 200,
    89→            dilationPixels: 3,
    90→          });
    91→
    92→          // Create final image with green background to prevent anti-aliased edge artifacts
    93→          const finalImageCanvas = createCanvasWithBackground(transformedCanvas, '#00FF00');
    94→
    95→          // Convert canvases to blobs and upload
    96→          const transformedBlob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
    97→            finalImageCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
    98→              if (blob) resolve(blob);
    99→              else reject(new Error('Failed to create transformed image blob'));
   100→            }, 'image/png');
   101→          });
   102→
   103→          const maskBlob = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
   104→            maskCanvas.toBlob(blob => {
   105→              if (blob) resolve(blob);
   106→              else reject(new Error('Failed to create mask blob'));
   107→            }, 'image/png');
   108→          });
   109→
   110→          // Upload transformed image and mask
   111→          const transformedFile = new File(
   112→            [transformedBlob],
   113→            `reposition_image_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`,
   114→            { type: 'image/png' }
   115→          );
   116→          const maskFile = new File(
   117→            [maskBlob],
   118→            `reposition_mask_${media.id}_${Date.now()}.png`,
   119→            { type: 'image/png' }
   120→          );
   121→
   122→          const [transformedUrl, maskUrl] = await Promise.all([
   123→            uploadImageToStorage(transformedFile),
   124→            uploadImageToStorage(maskFile)
   125→          ]);
   126→
   127→          // Create inpaint task with transformed image and mask
   128→          const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   129→          const effectivePrompt = inpaintPrompt.trim() || 'seamlessly extend and fill the edges matching the existing image style and content';
   130→
   131→          return createImageInpaintTask({
   132→            project_id: selectedProjectId,
   133→            image_url: transformedUrl,
   134→            mask_url: maskUrl,
   135→            prompt: effectivePrompt,
   136→            num_generations: inpaintNumGenerations,
   137→            generation_id: actualGenerationId ?? undefined,
   138→            shot_id: shotId,
   139→            tool_type: toolTypeOverride,
   140→            loras: loras,
   141→            create_as_generation: createAsGeneration,
   142→            source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
   143→            hires_fix: convertToHiresFixApiParams(advancedSettings),
   144→            qwen_edit_model: qwenEditModel,
   145→          });
   146→        },
   147→        onSuccess: () => {
   148→          setRepositionGenerateSuccess(true);
   149→          setTimeout(() => {
   150→            setRepositionGenerateSuccess(false);
   151→          }, 1000);
   152→        },
   153→      });
   154→    } finally {
   155→      setIsGeneratingReposition(false);
   156→    }
   157→  }, [
   158→    selectedProjectId,
   159→    imageDimensions,
   160→    hasTransformChanges,
   161→    media,
   162→    inpaintPrompt,
   163→    inpaintNumGenerations,
   164→    shotId,
   165→    toolTypeOverride,
   166→    loras,
   167→    createTransformedCanvas,
   168→    createAsGeneration,
   169→    advancedSettings,
   170→    activeVariantId,
   171→    qwenEditModel,
   172→    run,
   173→  ]);
   174→
   175→  return {
   176→    isGeneratingReposition,
   177→    repositionGenerateSuccess,
   178→    handleGenerateReposition,
   179→  };
   180→}
   181→
```

    81→function validateMagicEditParams(params: MagicEditTaskParams): void {

    83→

   106→function validateBatchMagicEditParams(params: BatchMagicEditTaskParams): void {
   107→  validateRequiredFields(params, ['project_id', 'prompt', 'image_url', 'numImages']);

   109→  // Validate numImages

   187→/**
   188→ * Creates a single magic edit task using the unified approach
   189→ * This replaces the direct call to the magic-edit edge function
   190→ * (internal use only - used by createBatchMagicEditTasks)
   191→ *
   192→ * @param params - Magic edit task parameters
   193→ * @returns Promise resolving to the created task
   194→ */
   195→async function createMagicEditTask(params: MagicEditTaskParams): Promise<TaskCreationResult> {
   196→  return runTaskCreationPipeline({
   197→    params,
   198→    context: 'MagicEdit',
   199→    validate: validateMagicEditParams,
   200→    buildTaskRequest: async (requestParams) => {
   201→      const { resolution: finalResolution } = await resolveProjectResolution(
   202→        requestParams.project_id,
   203→        requestParams.resolution,
   204→      );
   205→
   206→      const taskParams = buildMagicEditTaskParams(requestParams, finalResolution);
   207→
   208→      return composeTaskRequest({
   209→        source: requestParams,
   210→        taskType: "qwen_image_edit",
   211→        params: taskParams,
   212→      });
   213→    },
   214→  });
   215→}

   219→ * Used by MediaLightbox unified edit mode for creating multiple variations
   220→ * 
   221→ * @param params - Batch magic edit parameters

   223→ */
   224→export async function createBatchMagicEditTasks(params: BatchMagicEditTaskParams): Promise<TaskCreationResult[]> {

   226→  try {
   227→    return await runBatchTaskPipeline({
   228→      batchParams: params,

   232→          batchParams.project_id,
   233→          batchParams.resolution,
   234→        );

   237→          const seedBase = resolveSeed32Bit({ seed: batchParams.seed, field: 'seed' });
   238→          const seed = seedBase + index;
   239→
   240→          return {
   241→            project_id: batchParams.project_id,
   242→            prompt: batchParams.prompt,
   243→            image_url: batchParams.image_url,
   244→            negative_prompt: batchParams.negative_prompt,
   245→            resolution: finalResolution,
   246→            seed,
   247→            in_scene: batchParams.in_scene,
   248→            shot_id: batchParams.shot_id,
   249→            output_format: batchParams.output_format,
   250→            enable_sync_mode: batchParams.enable_sync_mode,
   251→            max_wait_seconds: batchParams.max_wait_seconds,
   252→            enable_base64_output: batchParams.enable_base64_output,
   253→            tool_type: batchParams.tool_type,
   254→            loras: batchParams.loras,

   256→            source_variant_id: batchParams.source_variant_id,
   257→            create_as_generation: batchParams.create_as_generation,
   258→            hires_fix: batchParams.hires_fix,
   259→            qwen_edit_model: batchParams.qwen_edit_model,
   260→          } as MagicEditTaskParams;
   261→        });
   262→      },
   263→      createSingleTask: createMagicEditTask,
   264→      operationName: 'createBatchMagicEditTasks',
   265→    });
   266→
   267→  } catch (error) {
   268→    rethrowTaskCreationError(error, { context: 'BatchMagicEdit' });
   269→  }
   270→}
