1→import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import {
     3→  createVideoEnhanceTask,
     4→  type VideoEnhanceTaskParams,
     5→} from '@/shared/lib/tasks/videoEnhance';
     6→import type { VideoEnhanceSettings } from './useGenerationEditSettings';
     7→import { useTaskPlaceholder } from '@/shared/hooks/useTaskPlaceholder';
     8→
     9→export type { VideoEnhanceSettings } from './useGenerationEditSettings';
    10→
    11→interface UseVideoEnhanceProps {
    12→  projectId: string | undefined;
    13→  videoUrl: string | undefined;
    14→  shotId?: string;
    15→  /** Generation ID to create variant on (for based_on relationship) */
    16→  generationId?: string;
    17→  /** Active variant ID - track which variant was enhanced */
    18→  activeVariantId?: string | null;
    19→  /** Settings from persistence system */
    20→  settings: VideoEnhanceSettings;
    21→  /** Setter for updating settings */
    22→  updateSettings: (updates: Partial<VideoEnhanceSettings>) => void;
    23→}
    24→
    25→interface UseVideoEnhanceReturn {
    26→  // Settings passed through for convenience
    27→  settings: VideoEnhanceSettings;
    28→  updateSetting: <K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
    29→    key: K,
    30→    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
    31→  ) => void;
    32→
    33→  // Validation
    34→  isValid: boolean;
    35→  canSubmit: boolean;
    36→
    37→  // Actions
    38→  handleGenerate: () => Promise<void>;
    39→
    40→  // Status
    41→  isGenerating: boolean;
    42→  generateSuccess: boolean;
    43→}
    44→
    45→/**
    46→ * Hook for managing video enhancement actions
    47→ *
    48→ * Settings are managed externally via the persistence system
    49→ * This hook handles validation and task creation
    50→ */
    51→export function useVideoEnhance({
    52→  projectId,
    53→  videoUrl,
    54→  shotId,
    55→  generationId,
    56→  activeVariantId,
    57→  settings,
    58→  updateSettings,
    59→}: UseVideoEnhanceProps): UseVideoEnhanceReturn {
    60→  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
    61→  const [generateSuccess, setGenerateSuccess] = useState(false);
    62→  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
    63→
    64→  // Adapter to match the expected interface
    65→  const updateSetting = useCallback(<K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
    66→    key: K,
    67→    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
    68→  ) => {
    69→    updateSettings({ [key]: value });
    70→    // Reset success state when settings change
    71→    setGenerateSuccess(false);
    72→  }, [updateSettings]);
    73→
    74→  // Validation: at least one mode must be enabled
    75→  const isValid = settings.enableInterpolation || settings.enableUpscale;
    76→
    77→  // Can submit: valid settings + required data
    78→  const canSubmit = isValid && !!projectId && !!videoUrl && !isGenerating;
    79→
    80→  // Handle generate action
    81→  const handleGenerate = useCallback(async () => {
    82→    if (!canSubmit || !projectId || !videoUrl) {
    83→      return;
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    setIsGenerating(true);
    87→    setGenerateSuccess(false);
    88→
    89→    const enhanceLabel = [
    90→      settings.enableInterpolation && 'Interpolation',
    91→      settings.enableUpscale && 'Upscale',
    92→    ].filter(Boolean).join(' + ');
    93→
    94→    try {
    95→      await run({
    96→        taskType: 'video_enhance',
    97→        label: enhanceLabel || 'Video Enhance',
    98→        context: 'useVideoEnhance',
    99→        toastTitle: 'Failed to create video enhancement task',
   100→        create: () => {
   101→          const params: VideoEnhanceTaskParams = {
   102→            project_id: projectId,
   103→            video_url: videoUrl,
   104→            enable_interpolation: settings.enableInterpolation,
   105→            enable_upscale: settings.enableUpscale,
   106→            shot_id: shotId,
   107→            based_on: generationId,
   108→            source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
   109→          };
   110→
   111→          if (settings.enableInterpolation) {
   112→            params.interpolation = {
   113→              num_frames: settings.numFrames,
   114→              use_calculated_fps: true,
   115→            };
   116→          }
   117→
   118→          if (settings.enableUpscale) {
   119→            params.upscale = {
   120→              upscale_factor: settings.upscaleFactor,
   121→              color_fix: settings.colorFix,
   122→              output_quality: settings.outputQuality,
   123→            };
   124→          }
   125→
   126→          return createVideoEnhanceTask(params);
   127→        },
   128→        onSuccess: () => {
   129→          setGenerateSuccess(true);
   130→        },
   131→      });
   132→    } finally {
   133→      setIsGenerating(false);
   134→    }
   135→  }, [
   136→    canSubmit,
   137→    projectId,
   138→    videoUrl,
   139→    shotId,
   140→    generationId,
   141→    activeVariantId,
   142→    settings,
   143→    run,
   144→  ]);
   145→
   146→  return {
   147→    settings,
   148→    updateSetting,
   149→    isValid,
   150→    canSubmit,
   151→    handleGenerate,
   152→    isGenerating,
   153→    generateSuccess,
   154→  };
   155→}
   156→

    13→const NON_PRELOADABLE_URL_MARKERS = ['_joined_frame.jpg'] as const;
    14→
    15→/**
    16→ * Get the canonical generation ID from a media item.
    17→ * Handles both direct generations (id) and variants (generation_id).
    18→ * Also checks metadata.generation_id for compatibility with various data shapes.
    19→ *
    20→ * Priority: generation_id > metadata.generation_id > id
    21→ *
    22→ * @example
    23→ * // Instead of: (media as unknown).generation_id || media.id
    24→ * const genId = getGenerationId(media);
    25→ */
    26→export function getGenerationId(
    27→  media: MaybeHasGenerationId | null | undefined
    28→): string | null {
    29→  if (!media) return null;
    30→  const metadataGenerationId = typeof media.metadata?.generation_id === 'string'
    31→    ? media.metadata.generation_id
    32→    : null;
    33→  return media.generation_id || metadataGenerationId || media.id || null;
    34→}

    36→/**

    39→export function isPreloadableMediaUrl(url: string | null | undefined): boolean {

    42→  }
    43→

Perfect. Now let me create a comprehensive summary of my findings:

## Summary: Generation ID Flow for Edit-Images and Edit-Video Tools

I've traced the complete flow from media selection through task creation in both edit-images and edit-video tools. Here's what I found:

### **Edit-Images Tool (✓ CORRECT)**

1. **Media Selection**: `InlineEditView.tsx` receives `media: GenerationRow` prop
2. **State Hook**: `useInlineEditState` calls `resolveActualGenerationId(media)` which:
   - Handles shot_generation legacy aliases
   - Returns `media.generation_id || (!isShotGenerationRecord ? media.id : null)`
3. **Task Creation - Image Inpaint & Annotated Edit**:
   - `useTaskGeneration` gets `actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media)` (line 99)
   - Passes to task creation as `generation_id` param (line 171)
   - `createImageInpaintTask` & `createAnnotatedImageEditTask` **map this to `based_on`** in task params (lines 48 in both)
   
4. **Task Creation - Magic Edit**:
   - `useMagicEditMode` gets `actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media)` (line 203)
   - Passes directly as `based_on` param (line 216)
   - `createBatchMagicEditTasks` passes through correctly

5. **Task Creation - Img2Img**:
   - `useImg2ImgMode` gets `actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media)` (line 167)
   - Passes directly as `based_on` param (line 177)
   - `createBatchZImageTurboImageToImageTasks` receives it correctly

6. **Task Creation - Reposition**:
   - `useRepositionTaskCreation` gets `actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media)` (line 128)
   - Passes as `generation_id` param (line 137)
   - `createImageInpaintTask` maps to `based_on`

### **Edit-Video Tool (✓ CORRECT)**

1. **Media Selection**: `InlineEditVideoView.tsx` receives `media: GenerationRow` prop
2. **Enhance Mode**:
   - `useVideoEnhance` gets `generationId` prop from caller
   - Passes directly as `based_on` param (line 107)
   - `createVideoEnhanceTask` receives it correctly

3. **Replace Mode**:
   - `useReplaceMode` extracts `getGenerationId(media)` (line 9 import)
   - Sets both `parent_generation_id` and `based_on` to the same value in orchestrator details (lines 336-337)
   - Also sets them in task params (lines 350-351)
   - `createTask` receives them correctly

### **Key Finding: INCONSISTENCY IN EDIT-IMAGES APPROACH**

There's a **minor but non-critical inconsistency**:
- Most edit-image hooks call `getGenerationId(media)` themselves (inpaint, magic edit, img2img, reposition)
- But `useInlineEditState` pre-computes `resolveActualGenerationId` and the persistence layer uses this
- The two functions differ slightly in logic:
  - `resolveActualGenerationId`: Checks for shot_generation records, then `generation_id || id`
  - `getGenerationId`: Returns `generation_id || metadata.generation_id || id`

However, this doesn't cause a bug because:
1. When inpainting/annotating calls `getGenerationId(media)` directly, it gets the right ID
2. The persistence system (variants) uses `actualGenerationId` from `resolveActualGenerationId`, but that's for querying variants, not for creating tasks
3. The actual task creation uses `getGenerationId(media)` for inpaint/annotate, bypassing the persistence-layer ID

### **Verification Result: ✓ FLOW IS CORRECT**

All edit modes properly pass the generation ID as `based_on` in task params, which flows through to `complete_task` and creates variants correctly:

| Edit Mode | Passes to Task | Maps to `based_on` | Status |
|-----------|---|---|---|
| Inpaint | `generation_id` | Yes (in task creation) | ✓ |
| Annotated Edit | `generation_id` | Yes (in task creation) | ✓ |
| Magic Edit | `based_on` | Yes (direct) | ✓ |
| Img2Img | `based_on` | Yes (direct) | ✓ |
| Reposition | `generation_id` | Yes (in task creation) | ✓ |
| Video Enhance | `based_on` | Yes (direct) | ✓ |
| Video Replace | `based_on` | Yes (both direct and in params) | ✓ |

The flow is complete and functional. The media ID properly reaches `complete_task` as `based_on` for all edit operations, enabling correct variant creation.
